import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpInterceptorFn } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { catchError, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

export const authInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);
  const token = authService.getToken();

  let authReq = req;
  if (token && !authService.isTokenExpired(token)) {
    authReq = req.clone({
      setHeaders: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    });
  }

  return next(authReq).pipe(
    catchError((error: HttpErrorResponse) => {
      if (error.status === 401) {
        authService.logout();
        try {
          router.navigate(['/login'])?.catch?.(() => {});
        } catch {
          // ignore navigation errors in unit test environments without configured routes
        }
      }
      return throwError(() => error);
    })
  );
};

import { Injectable, InjectionToken, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, tap } from 'rxjs';

export interface UserContext {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  roles: string[];
  is_superadmin: boolean;
  permissions?: string[];
}

export interface AuthResponse {
  access_token: string;
  token_type: string;
  user?: UserContext;
}

export interface LoginRequest {
  username_or_email: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface JwtPayload {
  sub?: string;
  username?: string;
  email?: string;
  roles?: string[];
  is_superadmin?: boolean;
  permissions?: string[];
  exp?: number;
  iat?: number;
  nbf?: number;
  jti?: string;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export const AUTH_STORAGE_KEY = 'datacatalog_access_token';

/**
 * Injection token for storage abstraction.
 * Falls back to an in-memory Storage implementation in environments where
 * window.localStorage is not accessible (e.g. Node 22+ runners, SSR).
 */
export const AUTH_STORAGE = new InjectionToken<Storage>('AUTH_STORAGE', {
  providedIn: 'root',
  factory: () => {
    try {
      if (
        typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
        window.localStorage &&
        typeof window.localStorage.getItem === 'function'
      ) {
        return window.localStorage;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall through to in-memory store
    }

    const store = new Map<string, string>();
    return {
      getItem: (key: string): string | null => store.get(key) ?? null,
      setItem: (key: string, value: string): void => {
        store.set(key, String(value));
      },
      removeItem: (key: string): void => {
        store.delete(key);
      },
      clear: (): void => {
        store.clear();
      },
      key: (index: number): string | null => Array.from(store.keys())[index] ?? null,
      get length(): number {
        return store.size;
      },
    } as Storage;
  },
});

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class AuthService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient, { optional: true });
  private readonly storage = inject(AUTH_STORAGE);

  private readonly _token = signal<string | null>(null);
  private readonly _currentUser = signal<UserContext | null>(null);

  public readonly token = this._token.asReadonly();
  public readonly currentUser = this._currentUser.asReadonly();

  public readonly isAuthenticated = computed(() => {
    const currentToken = this._token();
    return !!currentToken && !this.isTokenExpired(currentToken);
  });

  constructor() {
    this.restoreSessionFromStorage();
  }

  public getToken(): string | null {
    return this._token();
  }

  public setToken(token: string): void {
    if (!token) {
      this.logout();
      return;
    }

    const payload = this.decodePayload(token);
    if (!payload) {
      return;
    }

    const user: UserContext = {
      id: payload.sub ?? '',
      username: payload.username ?? '',
      email: payload.email ?? '',
      roles: Array.isArray(payload.roles) ? [...payload.roles] : [],
      is_superadmin: Boolean(payload.is_superadmin),
      permissions: Array.isArray(payload.permissions) ? [...payload.permissions] : [],
    };

    this._token.set(token);
    this._currentUser.set(user);
    this.storage.setItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY, token);
  }

  public logout(): void {
    this._token.set(null);
    this._currentUser.set(null);
    this.storage.removeItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
  }

  public login(credentials: LoginRequest): Observable<AuthResponse> {
    if (!this.http) {
      throw new Error('HttpClient is not available in the current injection context');
    }

    return this.http.post<AuthResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', credentials).pipe(
      tap((response: AuthResponse) => {
        if (response?.access_token) {
          this.setToken(response.access_token);
        }
      })
    );
  }

  public isTokenExpired(token?: string | null): boolean {
    const targetToken = token !== undefined ? token : this._token();
    if (!targetToken) {
      return true;
    }

    const payload = this.decodePayload(targetToken);
    if (!payload || typeof payload.exp !== 'number') {
      return true;
    }

    const currentEpochSeconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    return payload.exp <= currentEpochSeconds;
  }

  public decodePayload(token: string): JwtPayload | null {
    try {
      const parts = token.split('.');
      if (parts.length !== 3) {
        return null;
      }

      let base64 = parts[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
      while (base64.length % 4 !== 0) {
        base64 += '=';
      }

      const binaryString = atob(base64);
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
        bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
      }

      const decodedText = new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(bytes);
      return JSON.parse(decodedText) as JwtPayload;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private restoreSessionFromStorage(): void {
    const storedToken = this.storage.getItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!storedToken) {
      return;
    }

    if (!this.isTokenExpired(storedToken)) {
      this.setToken(storedToken);
    } else {
      this.storage.removeItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
    }
  }
}

export const ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  SUPERADMIN: ALL_PERMISSION_ACTIONS,
  ADMIN: ALL_PERMISSION_ACTIONS,
  DATA_STEWARD: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EDIT_DESCRIPTION,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_TAGS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATASTEWARD: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EDIT_DESCRIPTION,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_TAGS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATA_ENGINEER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_CONNECTIONS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATAENGINEER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_CONNECTIONS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  ANALYST: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATAANALYST: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATA_ANALYST: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  VIEWER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
  ],
  CATALOG_VIEWER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
  ],
};

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class RbacService {
  private readonly authService = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly _customPermissions = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  public readonly customPermissions = this._customPermissions.asReadonly();

  /**
   * Computed reactive signal containing all effective uppercase permission strings
   * resolved from superadmin status, explicit user permissions, assigned roles,
   * and custom overrides.
   */
  public readonly effectivePermissions = computed<Set<string>>(() => {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return new Set();
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return new Set(ALL_PERMISSION_ACTIONS.map(action => action.toUpperCase()));
    }

    const resolved = new Set<string>();

    // Add permissions from user context (e.g. from token)
    if (Array.isArray(user.permissions)) {
      for (const perm of user.permissions) {
        if (typeof perm === 'string' && perm.trim()) {
          resolved.add(perm.trim().toUpperCase());
        }
      }
    }

    // Add permissions mapped from user roles
    if (Array.isArray(user.roles)) {
      for (const role of user.roles) {
        const normalizedRole = role?.trim().toUpperCase() ?? '';
        const rolePerms = ROLE_PERMISSIONS[normalizedRole];
        if (Array.isArray(rolePerms)) {
          for (const perm of rolePerms) {
            resolved.add(perm.toUpperCase());
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Add dynamic custom permissions
    for (const perm of this._customPermissions()) {
      resolved.add(perm.toUpperCase());
    }

    return resolved;
  });

  /**
   * Checks whether the current user has the required permission (or all permissions if an array is provided).
   * Reactive when invoked inside an Angular effect() or computed().
   */
  public hasPermission(action: string | string[]): boolean {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return false;
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return true;
    }

    const permissions = this.effectivePermissions();

    if (Array.isArray(action)) {
      if (action.length === 0) {
        return true;
      }
      return action.every(a => typeof a === 'string' && permissions.has(a.trim().toUpperCase()));
    }

    if (typeof action !== 'string' || !action.trim()) {
      return false;
    }

    return permissions.has(action.trim().toUpperCase());
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether the current user has ANY of the specified permissions.
   */
  public hasAnyPermission(actions: string[]): boolean {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return false;
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(actions) || actions.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }

    const permissions = this.effectivePermissions();
    return actions.some(a => typeof a === 'string' && permissions.has(a.trim().toUpperCase()));
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether the current user has a specific role or any role from the provided list.
   */
  public hasRole(role: string | string[]): boolean {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return false;
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return true;
    }

    const userRoles = new Set((user.roles ?? []).map(r => r.toUpperCase()));

    if (Array.isArray(role)) {
      return role.some(r => userRoles.has(r.trim().toUpperCase()));
    }

    return userRoles.has(role.trim().toUpperCase());
  }

  /**
   * Sets custom dynamic permissions overriding or augmenting role-based permissions.
   */
  public setPermissions(permissions: string[]): void {
    const normalized = new Set(
      permissions
        .filter(p => typeof p === 'string' && p.trim().length > 0)
        .map(p => p.trim().toUpperCase())
    );
    this._customPermissions.set(normalized);
  }

  /**
   * Grants a single permission dynamically.
   */
  public grantPermission(permission: string): void {
    if (!permission || typeof permission !== 'string') {
      return;
    }
    const current = new Set(this._customPermissions());
    current.add(permission.trim().toUpperCase());
    this._customPermissions.set(current);
  }

  /**
   * Revokes a single dynamic permission.
   */
  public revokePermission(permission: string): void {
    if (!permission || typeof permission !== 'string') {
      return;
    }
    const current = new Set(this._customPermissions());
    current.delete(permission.trim().toUpperCase());
    this._customPermissions.set(current);
  }

  /**
   * Clears all custom permissions.
   */
  public clearPermissions(): void {
    this._customPermissions.set(new Set());
  }
}


=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\core\directives\has-permission.directive.ts ===
import {
  Directive,
  Input,
  TemplateRef,
  ViewContainerRef,
  effect,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { RbacService } from '../auth/rbac.service';

/**
 * Structural directive conditionally rendering DOM elements based on RBAC permissions.
 *
 * Driven reactively by Angular Signals:
 * Re-evaluates automatically when user authentication, role assignment, or input parameters change.
 *
 * Usage:
 * ```html
 * <button *hasPermission="'EDIT_DESCRIPTION'">Edit Table</button>
 *
 * <div *hasPermission="['VIEW_METADATA', 'MANAGE_TAGS']">Governance Controls</div>
 *
 * <div *hasPermission="'VIEW_AUDIT_LOGS'; else accessDenied">Audit Log</div>
 * <ng-template #accessDenied><span>Access Restricted</span></ng-template>
 * ```
 */
@Directive({
  selector: '[hasPermission]',
  standalone: true,
})
export class HasPermissionDirective {
  private readonly rbacService = inject(RbacService);
  private readonly templateRef = inject(TemplateRef<unknown>);
  private readonly viewContainer = inject(ViewContainerRef);

  private readonly _action = signal<string | string[]>('');
  private readonly _elseTemplate = signal<TemplateRef<unknown> | null>(null);

  private currentView: 'main' | 'else' | 'none' = 'none';

  @Input('hasPermission')
  set hasPermission(action: string | string[] | null | undefined) {
    this._action.set(action ?? '');
  }

  @Input('hasPermissionElse')
  set hasPermissionElse(template: TemplateRef<unknown> | null | undefined) {
    this._elseTemplate.set(template ?? null);
  }

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      const action = this._action();
      const elseTpl = this._elseTemplate();

      // Read effectivePermissions to establish reactive dependency on RBAC state
      const allowed = this.rbacService.hasPermission(action);

      if (allowed) {
        if (this.currentView !== 'main') {
          this.viewContainer.clear();
          this.viewContainer.createEmbeddedView(this.templateRef);
          this.currentView = 'main';
        }
      } else if (elseTpl) {
        if (this.currentView !== 'else') {
          this.viewContainer.clear();
          this.viewContainer.createEmbeddedView(elseTpl);
          this.currentView = 'else';
        }
      } else {
        if (this.currentView !== 'none') {
          this.viewContainer.clear();
          this.currentView = 'none';
        }
      }
    });
  }
}

import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../auth/auth.service';

export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = (_route, _state) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  if (authService.isAuthenticated()) {
    return true;
  }

  return router.parseUrl('/login');
};

import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
  CanActivateFn,
  Router,
  RouterStateSnapshot,
  UrlTree,
} from '@angular/router';
import { RbacService } from '../auth/rbac.service';

/**
 * Functional permission route guard enforcing RBAC authorization.
 *
 * Supports two usage patterns:
 * 1. Factory invocation in route config:
 *    `canActivate: [permissionGuard('EDIT_DESCRIPTION')]`
 *    `canActivate: [permissionGuard(['VIEW_METADATA', 'MANAGE_TAGS'])]`
 *
 * 2. Direct CanActivateFn registration relying on route data:
 *    `canActivate: [permissionGuard]`
 *    `data: { permission: 'EDIT_DESCRIPTION' }`
 */
export function permissionGuard(requiredAction: string | string[]): CanActivateFn;
export function permissionGuard(
  route: ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
  state: RouterStateSnapshot
): boolean | UrlTree;
export function permissionGuard(
  arg1: string | string[] | ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
  arg2?: RouterStateSnapshot
): CanActivateFn | boolean | UrlTree {
  if (typeof arg1 === 'string' || Array.isArray(arg1)) {
    const requiredAction = arg1;
    return (
      _route: ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
      _state: RouterStateSnapshot
    ): boolean | UrlTree => {
      const rbacService = inject(RbacService);
      const router = inject(Router);

      if (rbacService.hasPermission(requiredAction)) {
        return true;
      }
      return router.parseUrl('/unauthorized');
    };
  }

  // Invoked directly by the Angular Router as CanActivateFn
  const route = arg1 as ActivatedRouteSnapshot;
  const rbacService = inject(RbacService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const requiredAction = (route.data?.['permission'] ??
    route.data?.['action'] ??
    route.data?.['requiredAction']) as string | string[] | undefined;

  if (!requiredAction || rbacService.hasPermission(requiredAction)) {
    return true;
  }

  return router.parseUrl('/unauthorized');
}


=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\core\services\drift.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

export type DriftResolutionAction = 'RETAIN_APPROVED' | 'OVERWRITE_WITH_SOURCE' | 'CUSTOM_MERGE';

export interface ColumnDriftDetail {
  column_id: string;
  column_name: string;
  source_description?: string | null;
  approved_description?: string | null;
  drift_detected: boolean;
}

export interface DescriptionDriftDetailResponse {
  table_id: string;
  table_name: string;
  table_source_description?: string | null;
  table_approved_description?: string | null;
  drift_detected: boolean;
  column_drifts: ColumnDriftDetail[];
}

export interface ColumnDriftResolutionRequest {
  column_id: string;
  resolution_action: DriftResolutionAction;
  custom_description?: string | null;
}

export interface ResolveDescriptionDriftRequest {
  resolution_action: DriftResolutionAction;
  custom_description?: string | null;
  column_resolutions?: ColumnDriftResolutionRequest[];
}

export interface DescriptionDriftResolutionResponse {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  resolved_at: string;
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class DriftService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly tablesUrl = '/api/v1/tables';

  /**
   * Returns side-by-side comparison between native source database comment
   * and curated approved description for a table and its columns.
   */
  getTableDrift(tableId: string): Observable<DescriptionDriftDetailResponse> {
    return this.http.get<DescriptionDriftDetailResponse>(
      `${this.tablesUrl}/${tableId}/drift`
    );
  }

  /**
   * Resolves description drift on a table and optionally on columns by choosing an action:
   * RETAIN_APPROVED, OVERWRITE_WITH_SOURCE, or CUSTOM_MERGE. Resets drift flag.
   */
  resolveDrift(
    tableId: string,
    payload: ResolveDescriptionDriftRequest
  ): Observable<DescriptionDriftResolutionResponse> {
    return this.http.post<DescriptionDriftResolutionResponse>(
      `${this.tablesUrl}/${tableId}/drift/resolve`,
      payload
    );
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\core\services\sync-job.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

export type SyncJobStatus = 'QUEUED' | 'RUNNING' | 'SUCCESS' | 'PARTIAL_FAILURE' | 'FAILED';
export type SyncTriggerType = 'SCHEDULE' | 'MANUAL' | 'RETRY';
export type CircuitState = 'CLOSED' | 'OPEN' | 'HALF_OPEN';

export interface ConnectionDiagnosticTestResponse {
  success: boolean;
  latency_ms: number;
  dialect: string;
  server_version?: string | null;
  message: string;
  diagnostic_details?: Record<string, any> | null;
  tested_at: string;
}

export interface SyncJobResponse {
  id: string;
  connection_id: string;
  schedule_id?: string | null;
  status: SyncJobStatus;
  triggered_by: SyncTriggerType;
  started_at?: string | null;
  completed_at?: string | null;
  duration_ms?: number | null;
  tables_scanned: number;
  tables_created: number;
  tables_updated: number;
  tables_deleted: number;
  columns_scanned: number;
  changes_detected: number;
  error_message?: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface SyncJobDetailResponse extends SyncJobResponse {
  connection_name?: string | null;
  error_trace_jsonb: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface SyncJobListResponse {
  items: SyncJobResponse[];
  total: number;
  next_cursor?: string | null;
}

export interface CircuitBreakerStatusResponse {
  connection_id: string;
  circuit_state: CircuitState;
  consecutive_failures: number;
  cooldown_until?: string | null;
}

// Re-export drift and stats interfaces for convenience
export type {
  DriftResolutionAction,
  ColumnDriftDetail,
  DescriptionDriftDetailResponse,
  ColumnDriftResolutionRequest,
  ResolveDescriptionDriftRequest,
  DescriptionDriftResolutionResponse,
} from './drift.service';

export type {
  TableStatisticPoint,
  TableStatisticsHistoryResponse,
} from './table-stats.service';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class SyncJobService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly connectionsUrl = '/api/v1/connections';
  private readonly syncJobsUrl = '/api/v1/sync-jobs';

  /**
   * Executes a transient dialect connectivity diagnostic test bounded by a 5.0s timeout.
   */
  testConnection(connectionId: string): Observable<ConnectionDiagnosticTestResponse> {
    return this.http.post<ConnectionDiagnosticTestResponse>(
      `${this.connectionsUrl}/${connectionId}/test`,
      {}
    );
  }

  /**
   * Triggers immediate synchronization of database metadata.
   */
  triggerSync(connectionId: string): Observable<SyncJobResponse> {
    return this.http.post<SyncJobResponse>(
      `${this.connectionsUrl}/${connectionId}/sync`,
      {}
    );
  }

  /**
   * Resets tripped circuit breaker back to CLOSED state.
   */
  resetCircuitBreaker(connectionId: string): Observable<CircuitBreakerStatusResponse> {
    return this.http.post<CircuitBreakerStatusResponse>(
      `${this.connectionsUrl}/${connectionId}/circuit-breaker/reset`,
      {}
    );
  }

  /**
   * Queries global sync execution history with status filters and cursor pagination.
   */
  getSyncJobs(
    status?: string,
    connectionId?: string,
    cursor?: string,
    limit: number = 50
  ): Observable<SyncJobListResponse> {
    let params = new HttpParams().set('limit', limit.toString());
    if (status && status !== 'ALL') {
      params = params.set('status', status);
    }
    if (connectionId) {
      params = params.set('connection_id', connectionId);
    }
    if (cursor) {
      params = params.set('cursor', cursor);
    }

    return this.http.get<SyncJobListResponse>(this.syncJobsUrl, { params });
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves full sync job details including schema-level partial failures and error traces.
   */
  getSyncJobDetail(jobId: string): Observable<SyncJobDetailResponse> {
    return this.http.get<SyncJobDetailResponse>(`${this.syncJobsUrl}/${jobId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Re-enqueues a failed sync job with RETRY trigger.
   */
  retrySyncJob(jobId: string): Observable<SyncJobResponse> {
    return this.http.post<SyncJobResponse>(
      `${this.syncJobsUrl}/${jobId}/retry`,
      {}
    );
  }

  /**
   * Lists sync job execution history specifically for a connection.
   */
  getConnectionSyncJobs(
    connectionId: string,
    limit: number = 20,
    cursor?: string
  ): Observable<SyncJobListResponse> {
    let params = new HttpParams().set('limit', limit.toString());
    if (cursor) {
      params = params.set('cursor', cursor);
    }

    return this.http.get<SyncJobListResponse>(
      `${this.connectionsUrl}/${connectionId}/jobs`,
      { params }
    );
  }
}

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

export interface TableStatisticPoint {
  recorded_at: string;
  row_count_estimate: number;
  size_bytes: number;
  sync_job_id?: string | null;
}

export interface TableStatisticsHistoryResponse {
  table_id: string;
  data_points: TableStatisticPoint[];
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class TableStatsService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly tablesUrl = '/api/v1/tables';

  /**
   * Retrieves time-series history of estimated row counts and size in bytes
   * from table_statistics_history without structural version clutter.
   */
  getTableStatistics(
    tableId: string,
    limit: number = 30
  ): Observable<TableStatisticsHistoryResponse> {
    const params = new HttpParams().set('limit', limit.toString());
    return this.http.get<TableStatisticsHistoryResponse>(
      `${this.tablesUrl}/${tableId}/statistics`,
      { params }
    );
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\access-control\db-users\db-user-monitor.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ConnectionService } from '../../connections/services/connection.service';
import { DatabaseConnection } from '../../connections/models/connection.models';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../../catalog/models/catalog.models';
import { DbUserService } from '../services/db-user.service';
import { DatabaseUserItem } from '../models/db-user.models';
import { HasPermissionDirective } from '../../../core/directives/has-permission.directive';

export type UserFilterType = 'ALL' | 'LOGIN' | 'ROLES';

export interface DbTreeCategoryNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  connections: DatabaseConnection[];
  children: DbTreeCategoryNode[];
  hasMatchingDescendants: boolean;
  totalDatabasesCount: number;
  isUncategorized?: boolean;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-db-user-monitor',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterModule, HasPermissionDirective],
  templateUrl: './db-user-monitor.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./db-user-monitor.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class DbUserMonitorComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly connectionService = inject(ConnectionService);
  private readonly dbUserService = inject(DbUserService);
  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute);

  // Notifications
  successMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Backend state
  categories = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  connections = signal<DatabaseConnection[]>([]);
  isLoadingConnections = signal<boolean>(false);
  selectedConnection = signal<DatabaseConnection | null>(null);

  // Multi-criteria tree filtering
  filterConnectionName = signal<string>('');
  filterDatabaseName = signal<string>('');
  filterHost = signal<string>('');
  filterOwner = signal<string>('');
  filterEngineType = signal<string>('');

  // Tree expansion state
  expandedCategoryIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set<string>());

  // Users list state
  users = signal<DatabaseUserItem[]>([]);
  isLoadingUsers = signal<boolean>(false);
  userSearchQuery = signal<string>('');
  userTypeFilter = signal<UserFilterType>('ALL');

  // Check whether any tree filter is active
  hasActiveTreeFilters = computed<boolean>(() => {
    return Boolean(
      this.filterConnectionName().trim() ||
      this.filterDatabaseName().trim() ||
      this.filterHost().trim() ||
      this.filterOwner().trim() ||
      this.filterEngineType()
    );
  });

  // Predicate to verify if a connection matches all active multi-criteria filters
  matchesConnection(conn: DatabaseConnection): boolean {
    const nameQ = this.filterConnectionName().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (nameQ && !conn.name.toLowerCase().includes(nameQ)) {
      return false;
    }

    const dbNameQ = this.filterDatabaseName().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (dbNameQ && !conn.database_name.toLowerCase().includes(dbNameQ)) {
      return false;
    }

    const hostQ = this.filterHost().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (hostQ && !conn.host.toLowerCase().includes(hostQ)) {
      return false;
    }

    const ownerQ = this.filterOwner().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (ownerQ && (!conn.owner || !conn.owner.toLowerCase().includes(ownerQ))) {
      return false;
    }

    const engine = this.filterEngineType();
    if (engine && conn.engine_type.toUpperCase() !== engine.toUpperCase()) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  }

  // Hierarchical Category -> Subcategories -> Databases Tree
  readonly displayTree = computed<DbTreeCategoryNode[]>(() => {
    const rawCategories = this.categories();
    const allConns = this.connections();
    const activeFilters = this.hasActiveTreeFilters();
    const connMap = new Map<string, DatabaseConnection>(allConns.map((c) => [c.id, c]));

    const buildCategoryNode = (cat: CategoryTreeNode): DbTreeCategoryNode => {
      const assigned: DatabaseConnection[] = [];
      const seenIds = new Set<string>();

      if (cat.databases) {
        for (const dbInfo of cat.databases) {
          const c = connMap.get(dbInfo.id);
          if (c && !seenIds.has(c.id)) {
            seenIds.add(c.id);
            assigned.push(c);
          }
        }
      }

      for (const c of allConns) {
        if (c.category_id === cat.id && !seenIds.has(c.id)) {
          seenIds.add(c.id);
          assigned.push(c);
        }
      }

      const matchingConnections = activeFilters
        ? assigned.filter((c) => this.matchesConnection(c))
        : assigned;

      const matchingChildren: DbTreeCategoryNode[] = [];
      for (const child of cat.children || []) {
        const childNode = buildCategoryNode(child);
        if (!activeFilters || childNode.hasMatchingDescendants || childNode.connections.length > 0) {
          matchingChildren.push(childNode);
        }
      }

      const hasMatch =
        matchingConnections.length > 0 ||
        matchingChildren.some((child) => child.hasMatchingDescendants || child.connections.length > 0);

      const totalDbs =
        assigned.length +
        matchingChildren.reduce((acc, child) => acc + child.totalDatabasesCount, 0);

      return {
        id: cat.id,
        name: cat.name,
        description: cat.description,
        connections: matchingConnections,
        children: matchingChildren,
        hasMatchingDescendants: hasMatch,
        totalDatabasesCount: totalDbs,
      };
    };

    const tree: DbTreeCategoryNode[] = [];
    for (const cat of rawCategories) {
      const node = buildCategoryNode(cat);
      if (!activeFilters || node.hasMatchingDescendants || node.connections.length > 0) {
        tree.push(node);
      }
    }

    return tree;
  });

  // Automatic آ«ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡آ» branch for uncategorized databases
  readonly uncategorizedNode = computed<DbTreeCategoryNode | null>(() => {
    const rawCategories = this.categories();
    const allConns = this.connections();
    const activeFilters = this.hasActiveTreeFilters();

    const assignedIds = new Set<string>();
    const collectAssignedIds = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[]) => {
      for (const cat of nodes) {
        if (cat.databases) {
          for (const db of cat.databases) {
            assignedIds.add(db.id);
          }
        }
        if (cat.children) {
          collectAssignedIds(cat.children);
        }
      }
    };
    collectAssignedIds(rawCategories);

    for (const c of allConns) {
      if (c.category_id) {
        assignedIds.add(c.id);
      }
    }

    const uncatConns = allConns.filter((c) => !assignedIds.has(c.id));
    if (uncatConns.length === 0) return null;

    const matchingUncat = activeFilters
      ? uncatConns.filter((c) => this.matchesConnection(c))
      : uncatConns;

    if (activeFilters && matchingUncat.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return {
      id: 'uncategorized',
      name: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡',
      description: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ ظ‡غŒع† ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹غŒ ظ…ظ†طھط³ط¨ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯',
      connections: matchingUncat,
      children: [],
      hasMatchingDescendants: matchingUncat.length > 0,
      totalDatabasesCount: uncatConns.length,
      isUncategorized: true,
    };
  });

  // Total matching databases count across visible tree
  totalFilteredDatabasesCount = computed<number>(() => {
    const tree = this.displayTree();
    const uncat = this.uncategorizedNode();
    let sum = 0;
    const countConns = (node: DbTreeCategoryNode) => {
      sum += node.connections.length;
      for (const ch of node.children) {
        countConns(ch);
      }
    };
    for (const root of tree) {
      countConns(root);
    }
    if (uncat) {
      sum += uncat.connections.length;
    }
    return sum;
  });

  // Determines whether a category node should be expanded (auto-expanded when searching)
  isCategoryExpanded(node: DbTreeCategoryNode): boolean {
    if (this.hasActiveTreeFilters()) {
      return node.hasMatchingDescendants || node.connections.length > 0;
    }
    return this.expandedCategoryIds().has(node.id);
  }

  // Toggles expand/collapse state for a category node
  toggleCategory(node: DbTreeCategoryNode, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    const currentlyExpanded = this.isCategoryExpanded(node);
    const next = new Set(this.expandedCategoryIds());
    if (currentlyExpanded) {
      next.delete(node.id);
    } else {
      next.add(node.id);
    }
    this.expandedCategoryIds.set(next);
  }

  // Resets all tree multi-criteria search filters
  clearTreeFilters(): void {
    this.filterConnectionName.set('');
    this.filterDatabaseName.set('');
    this.filterHost.set('');
    this.filterOwner.set('');
    this.filterEngineType.set('');
  }

  // Filtered users list with clarified filter pills
  filteredUsers = computed<DatabaseUserItem[]>(() => {
    const query = this.userSearchQuery().trim().toLowerCase();
    const filter = this.userTypeFilter();
    let list = this.users();

    if (filter === 'LOGIN') {
      list = list.filter((u) => u.can_login);
    } else if (filter === 'ROLES') {
      list = list.filter((u) => !u.can_login);
    }

    if (query) {
      list = list.filter(
        (u) =>
          u.username.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          u.member_of_roles.some((r) => r.toLowerCase().includes(query))
      );
    }

    return list;
  });

  // Counts for summary pills
  userStats = computed(() => {
    const all = this.users();
    return {
      total: all.length,
      loginUsers: all.filter((u) => u.can_login).length,
      roles: all.filter((u) => !u.can_login).length,
      activeSessions: all.reduce((acc, u) => acc + (u.active_sessions || 0), 0),
    };
  });

  // Confirmation modal state for Login Toggle
  toggleLoginTarget = signal<DatabaseUserItem | null>(null);
  isTogglingLogin = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Edit settings modal state
  editTarget = signal<DatabaseUserItem | null>(null);
  editConnectionLimit = signal<number>(-1);
  editValidUntil = signal<string>('');
  editIsUnlimited = signal<boolean>(true);
  isSavingSettings = signal<boolean>(false);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadData();
  }

  loadData(): void {
    this.isLoadingConnections.set(true);
    forkJoin({
      categories: this.connectionService.getCategoryTree(),
      connections: this.connectionService.getConnections(),
    }).subscribe({
      next: ({ categories, connections }) => {
        this.categories.set(categories);
        this.connections.set(connections);
        this.isLoadingConnections.set(false);

        // Auto-expand all top-level categories and uncategorized branch initially
        const initialExpanded = new Set<string>();
        categories.forEach((c) => initialExpanded.add(c.id));
        initialExpanded.add('uncategorized');
        this.expandedCategoryIds.set(initialExpanded);

        // Check if query param specifies connectionId
        const targetId = this.route.snapshot.queryParams['connectionId'];
        if (targetId) {
          const match = connections.find((c) => c.id === targetId);
          if (match) {
            this.selectConnection(match);
            return;
          }
        }

        // Auto-select first PostgreSQL connection or first connection
        if (connections.length > 0) {
          const firstPg = connections.find((c) => c.engine_type === 'POSTGRESQL') || connections[0];
          this.selectConnection(firstPg);
        }
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoadingConnections.set(false);
        this.showError('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¯ط±غŒط§ظپطھ ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§ ظˆ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡: ' + (err.error?.detail || err.message));
      },
    });
  }

  selectConnection(conn: DatabaseConnection): void {
    this.selectedConnection.set(conn);
    this.loadUsers(conn.id);
  }

  loadUsers(connectionId: string): void {
    this.isLoadingUsers.set(true);
    this.users.set([]);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.dbUserService.getDatabaseUsers(connectionId).subscribe({
      next: (dbUsers) => {
        this.users.set(dbUsers);
        this.isLoadingUsers.set(false);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoadingUsers.set(false);
        this.showError('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¯ط±غŒط§ظپطھ ظ„غŒط³طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط§ظ† ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡: ' + (err.error?.detail || err.message));
      },
    });
  }

  refreshCurrentUsers(): void {
    const conn = this.selectedConnection();
    if (conn) {
      this.loadUsers(conn.id);
    }
  }

  // --- Toggle Login Modal ---
  openToggleLoginModal(user: DatabaseUserItem): void {
    this.toggleLoginTarget.set(user);
  }

  closeToggleLoginModal(): void {
    this.toggleLoginTarget.set(null);
  }

  confirmToggleLogin(): void {
    const user = this.toggleLoginTarget();
    const conn = this.selectedConnection();
    if (!user || !conn) return;

    const newLoginState = !user.can_login;
    this.isTogglingLogin.set(true);

    this.dbUserService
      .updateDatabaseUser(conn.id, user.username, { can_login: newLoginState })
      .subscribe({
        next: (res) => {
          this.isTogglingLogin.set(false);
          this.closeToggleLoginModal();
          this.showSuccess(
            `ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ظˆط±ظˆط¯ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${user.username}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ${newLoginState ? 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„' : 'ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„'} ط´ط¯.`
          );
          this.refreshCurrentUsers();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isTogglingLogin.set(false);
          this.showError(err.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ظˆط±ظˆط¯ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±.');
        },
      });
  }

  // --- Edit Settings Modal ---
  openEditModal(user: DatabaseUserItem): void {
    this.editTarget.set(user);
    this.editConnectionLimit.set(user.connection_limit ?? -1);
    if (user.valid_until && user.valid_until.toLowerCase() !== 'infinity') {
      this.editIsUnlimited.set(false);
      const datePart = user.valid_until.split('T')[0].split(' ')[0];
      this.editValidUntil.set(datePart);
    } else {
      this.editIsUnlimited.set(true);
      this.editValidUntil.set('');
    }
  }

  closeEditModal(): void {
    this.editTarget.set(null);
  }

  saveSettings(): void {
    const user = this.editTarget();
    const conn = this.selectedConnection();
    if (!user || !conn) return;

    this.isSavingSettings.set(true);
    const validUntilValue = this.editIsUnlimited() ? 'infinity' : this.editValidUntil();

    this.dbUserService
      .updateDatabaseUser(conn.id, user.username, {
        connection_limit: this.editConnectionLimit(),
        valid_until: validUntilValue,
      })
      .subscribe({
        next: (res) => {
          this.isSavingSettings.set(false);
          this.closeEditModal();
          this.showSuccess(`طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${user.username}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط¨ظ‡â€Œط±ظˆط²ط±ط³ط§ظ†غŒ ط´ط¯.`);
          this.refreshCurrentUsers();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isSavingSettings.set(false);
          this.showError(err.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±.');
        },
      });
  }

  formatDate(dateStr: string | null): string {
    if (!dateStr || dateStr.toLowerCase() === 'infinity') {
      return 'ظ†ط§ظ…ط­ط¯ظˆط¯ (ط¯ط§ط¦ظ…غŒ)';
    }
    try {
      const d = new Date(dateStr);
      if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return dateStr;
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR', {
        year: 'numeric',
        month: '2-digit',
        day: '2-digit',
      }).format(d);
    } catch {
      return dateStr;
    }
  }

  private showSuccess(msg: string): void {
    this.successMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.successMessage.set(null), 5000);
  }

  private showError(msg: string): void {
    this.errorMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.errorMessage.set(null), 8000);
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from "@angular/core";

import { FormsModule } from "@angular/forms";
import { RouterModule } from "@angular/router";
import { HasPermissionDirective } from "../../../core/directives/has-permission.directive";
import { RoleManagementService } from "../services/role-management.service";
import {
  PermissionDefinition,
  PermissionModule,
  RoleCreateDto,
  RoleItem,
  RoleUpdateDto,
  RoleUserItem,
} from "../models/access-control.models";

export interface FlatPermissionItem {
  moduleKey: string;
  moduleName: string;
  action: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: "app-role-management",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, RouterModule, HasPermissionDirective],
  templateUrl: "./role-management.component.html",
  styleUrls: ["./role-management.component.css"],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class RoleManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private roleService = inject(RoleManagementService);

  // Roles list state
  rawRoles = signal<RoleItem[]>([]);
  currentPage = signal<number>(1);
  pageSize = signal<number>(5);
  availablePageSizes = [5, 10, 20, 50];

  // Column search filters & Unified search
  searchTerm = signal<string>("");
  filterPersianName = signal<string>("");
  filterEnglishName = signal<string>("");

  isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSaving = signal<boolean>(false);
  errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  successMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Master-Detail selection & Form state
  selectedRole = signal<RoleItem | null>(null);
  isCreateMode = signal<boolean>(false);
  isEditMode = signal<boolean>(false);
  isFormEditable = computed<boolean>(
    () => this.isCreateMode() || this.isEditMode(),
  );

  // Form Fields (English system name & Persian display title)
  formEnglishName = signal<string>("");
  formPersianName = signal<string>("");

  // Sub-tabs in detail panel: 'permissions' | 'users'
  activeSubTab = signal<"permissions" | "users">("permissions");

  // Permissions registry & current role permissions
  permissionModules = signal<PermissionModule[]>([]);
  roleSelectedActions = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());
  permissionFilterQuery = signal<string>("");

  // Users assigned to selected role
  roleUsers = signal<RoleUserItem[]>([]);
  isLoadingRoleUsers = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Filtered and Paginated View Models
  filteredRoles = computed<RoleItem[]>(() => {
    const list = this.rawRoles();
    const search = this.searchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const faFilter = this.filterPersianName().trim().toLowerCase();
    const enFilter = this.filterEnglishName().trim().toLowerCase();

    return list.filter((r) => {
      const pName = (r.description || r.name).toLowerCase();
      const eName = r.name.toLowerCase();
      if (search && !pName.includes(search) && !eName.includes(search))
        return false;
      if (faFilter && !pName.includes(faFilter)) return false;
      if (enFilter && !eName.includes(enFilter)) return false;
      return true;
    });
  });

  paginatedRoles = computed<RoleItem[]>(() => {
    const all = this.filteredRoles();
    const start = (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize();
    return all.slice(start, start + this.pageSize());
  });

  totalPages = computed<number>(() => {
    const count = this.filteredRoles().length;
    return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(count / this.pageSize()));
  });

  pageDisplayStart = computed<number>(() => {
    if (this.filteredRoles().length === 0) return 0;
    return (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize() + 1;
  });

  pageDisplayEnd = computed<number>(() => {
    return Math.min(
      this.currentPage() * this.pageSize(),
      this.filteredRoles().length,
    );
  });

  // Flat list of permissions for table
  flatPermissions = computed<FlatPermissionItem[]>(() => {
    const query = this.permissionFilterQuery().trim().toLowerCase();
    const flat: FlatPermissionItem[] = [];

    for (const mod of this.permissionModules()) {
      for (const p of mod.permissions) {
        if (
          !query ||
          p.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          p.description.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          p.action.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          mod.module_name.toLowerCase().includes(query)
        ) {
          flat.push({
            moduleKey: mod.module_key,
            moduleName: mod.module_name,
            action: p.action,
            name: p.name,
            description: p.description,
          });
        }
      }
    }
    return flat;
  });

  readonly Math = Math;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadRoles();
    this.loadPermissionsRegistry();
  }

  loadRoles(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.roleService.getAllRolesList().subscribe({
      next: (roles) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.rawRoles.set(roles);

        const current = this.selectedRole();
        if (current) {
          const fresh = roles.find((r) => r.id === current.id);
          if (fresh) {
            this.selectRole(fresh);
          }
        } else if (roles.length > 0 && !this.isCreateMode()) {
          this.selectRole(roles[0]);
        }
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظپط±ط§ط®ظˆط§ظ†غŒ ظ„غŒط³طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§.");
      },
    });
  }

  loadPermissionsRegistry(): void {
    this.roleService.getPermissionsRegistry().subscribe({
      next: (modules) => this.permissionModules.set(modules),
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  selectRole(role: RoleItem): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(false);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedRole.set(role);
    this.formEnglishName.set(role.name);
    this.formPersianName.set(role.description || role.name);

    // Load permissions for this role
    this.roleService.getRolePermissions(role.id).subscribe({
      next: (rules) => {
        const actions = new Set<string>();
        rules.forEach((r) => actions.add(r.action));
        this.roleSelectedActions.set(actions);
      },
      error: () => this.roleSelectedActions.set(new Set()),
    });

    // Load users for this role
    this.loadRoleUsers(role.id);
  }

  startEditRole(role: RoleItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) {
      event.stopPropagation();
    }
    this.selectRole(role);
    this.isEditMode.set(true);
  }

  enableEditMode(): void {
    this.isEditMode.set(true);
  }

  loadRoleUsers(roleId: string): void {
    this.isLoadingRoleUsers.set(true);
    this.roleService.getRoleUsers(roleId).subscribe({
      next: (users) => {
        this.isLoadingRoleUsers.set(false);
        this.roleUsers.set(users);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingRoleUsers.set(false);
        this.roleUsers.set([]);
      },
    });
  }

  startCreateNewRole(): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(true);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedRole.set(null);
    this.formEnglishName.set("");
    this.formPersianName.set("");
    this.roleSelectedActions.set(new Set());
    this.roleUsers.set([]);
    this.activeSubTab.set("permissions");
  }

  cancelDetailEdit(): void {
    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      if (this.rawRoles().length > 0) {
        this.selectRole(this.rawRoles()[0]);
      } else {
        this.isCreateMode.set(false);
        this.selectedRole.set(null);
      }
    } else {
      const current = this.selectedRole();
      this.isEditMode.set(false);
      if (current) {
        this.selectRole(current);
      }
    }
  }

  onFilterChanged(): void {
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  goToPage(p: number): void {
    if (p >= 1 && p <= this.totalPages()) {
      this.currentPage.set(p);
    }
  }

  changePageSize(newSize: number): void {
    this.pageSize.set(newSize);
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  switchSubTab(tab: "permissions" | "users"): void {
    this.activeSubTab.set(tab);
  }

  // --- Checkbox toggle for permissions ---
  hasPermission(action: string): boolean {
    return this.roleSelectedActions().has(action);
  }

  togglePermission(action: string, event: Event): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) {
      return;
    }
    const checked = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
    const current = new Set(this.roleSelectedActions());
    if (checked) {
      current.add(action);
    } else {
      current.delete(action);
    }
    this.roleSelectedActions.set(current);
  }

  // --- Save / Submit Role ---
  saveRole(): void {
    const englishName = this.formEnglishName().trim();
    const persianName = this.formPersianName().trim();

    if (!englishName || !persianName) {
      this.showError(
        "ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ظ‡ظ… ظ†ط§ظ… ط§ظ†ع¯ظ„غŒط³غŒ (ع©ط¯ ط³غŒط³طھظ…غŒ) ظˆ ظ‡ظ… ظ†ط§ظ… ظپط§ط±ط³غŒ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.",
      );
      return;
    }

    this.isSaving.set(true);

    const permissionPayload = Array.from(this.roleSelectedActions()).map(
      (act) => ({
        resource_type: "GLOBAL",
        action: act,
        effect: "ALLOW",
      }),
    );

    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      const createDto: RoleCreateDto = {
        name: englishName,
        description: persianName,
      };

      this.roleService.createRole(createDto).subscribe({
        next: (created) => {
          this.roleService
            .updateRolePermissions(created.id, permissionPayload)
            .subscribe({
              next: () => {
                this.isSaving.set(false);
                this.isEditMode.set(false);
                this.showSuccess(
                  `ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ آ«${persianName}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯.`,
                );
                this.loadRoles();
                this.selectRole(created);
              },
              error: () => {
                this.isSaving.set(false);
                this.isEditMode.set(false);
                this.showSuccess(
                  `ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯طŒ ط§ظ…ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§ ط®ط·ط§غŒغŒ ط±ط® ط¯ط§ط¯.`,
                );
                this.loadRoles();
                this.selectRole(created);
              },
            });
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isSaving.set(false);
          this.showError(err?.error?.detail || "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط«ط¨طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯.");
        },
      });
    } else {
      const role = this.selectedRole();
      if (!role) {
        this.isSaving.set(false);
        return;
      }

      const updateDto: RoleUpdateDto = {
        name: englishName,
        description: persianName,
      };

      this.roleService.updateRole(role.id, updateDto).subscribe({
        next: (updated) => {
          this.roleService
            .updateRolePermissions(updated.id, permissionPayload)
            .subscribe({
              next: () => {
                this.isSaving.set(false);
                this.isEditMode.set(false);
                this.showSuccess(
                  `ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظˆ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§غŒ ظ†ظ‚ط´ آ«${persianName}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط¨ظ‡â€Œط±ظˆط²ط±ط³ط§ظ†غŒ ط´ط¯.`,
                );
                this.loadRoles();
              },
              error: () => {
                this.isSaving.set(false);
                this.isEditMode.set(false);
                this.showError(
                  "ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯ ط§ظ…ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§ ط®ط·ط§غŒغŒ ط±ط® ط¯ط§ط¯.",
                );
                this.loadRoles();
              },
            });
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isSaving.set(false);
          this.showError(err?.error?.detail || "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´.");
        },
      });
    }
  }

  // Delete confirmation dialog signal
  readonly roleToDelete = signal<RoleItem | null>(null);

  requestDeleteRole(role: RoleItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    if (role.is_system_role) {
      this.showError("ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط³غŒط³طھظ…غŒ ظˆ ظ¾غŒط´â€Œظپط±ط¶ ط³ط§ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‡ ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œط¨ط§ط´ظ†ط¯.");
      return;
    }
    this.roleToDelete.set(role);
  }

  cancelDeleteRole(): void {
    this.roleToDelete.set(null);
  }

  confirmDeleteRole(): void {
    const role = this.roleToDelete();
    if (!role) return;

    this.roleService.deleteRole(role.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.roleToDelete.set(null);
        this.showSuccess(
          `ظ†ظ‚ط´ آ«${role.description || role.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط­ط°ظپ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯.`,
        );
        if (this.selectedRole()?.id === role.id) {
          this.selectedRole.set(null);
        }
        this.loadRoles();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.roleToDelete.set(null);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ.");
      },
    });
  }

  deleteRole(role: RoleItem, event: Event): void {
    this.requestDeleteRole(role, event);
  }

  showSuccess(msg: string): void {
    this.successMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.successMessage.set(null), 4000);
  }

  showError(msg: string): void {
    this.errorMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.errorMessage.set(null), 5000);
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\access-control\services\db-user.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  DatabaseUserActionResponse,
  DatabaseUserItem,
  DatabaseUserUpdateRequest,
} from '../models/db-user.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class DbUserService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/connections';

  /**
   * Retrieves active database users and roles for a specific connection.
   */
  getDatabaseUsers(connectionId: string): Observable<DatabaseUserItem[]> {
    return this.http.get<DatabaseUserItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${connectionId}/db-users`);
  }

  /**
   * Updates database user login permission, connection limit, or expiration date.
   */
  updateDatabaseUser(
    connectionId: string,
    username: string,
    payload: DatabaseUserUpdateRequest
  ): Observable<DatabaseUserActionResponse> {
    const encodedUser = encodeURIComponent(username);
    return this.http.patch<DatabaseUserActionResponse>(
      `${this.baseUrl}/${connectionId}/db-users/${encodedUser}`,
      payload
    );
  }
}

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  DirectPermissionRule,
  GroupCreateDto,
  GroupItem,
  GroupMemberItem,
  GroupUpdateDto,
  PaginatedResponse,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class GroupManagementService {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private baseUrl = '/api/v1/groups';

  getGroups(params: {
    page?: number;
    page_size?: number;
    search?: string;
    is_active?: boolean;
  }): Observable<PaginatedResponse<GroupItem>> {
    let httpParams = new HttpParams();
    if (params.page !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page', params.page);

    if (params.search) httpParams = httpParams.set('search', params.search);
    if (params.is_active !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('is_active', params.is_active);

    return this.http.get<PaginatedResponse<GroupItem>>(this.baseUrl, { params: httpParams });
  }

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  DirectPermissionRule,
  EffectivePermissionRow,
  PaginatedResponse,
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  UserActivityItem,
  UserCreateDto,
  UserDetailData,
  UserItem,
  UserUpdateDto,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class UserManagementService {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private baseUrl = '/api/v1/users';

  getUsers(params: {
    page?: number;
    page_size?: number;
    search?: string;
    role_id?: string;
    group_id?: string;
    status?: string;
  }): Observable<PaginatedResponse<UserItem>> {
    let httpParams = new HttpParams();
    if (params.page !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page', params.page);
    if (params.page_size !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page_size', params.page_size);
    if (params.search) httpParams = httpParams.set('search', params.search);
    if (params.role_id) httpParams = httpParams.set('role_id', params.role_id);
    if (params.group_id) httpParams = httpParams.set('group_id', params.group_id);
    if (params.status) httpParams = httpParams.set('status', params.status);

    return this.http.get<PaginatedResponse<UserItem>>(this.baseUrl, { params: httpParams });
  }

  getUser(id: string): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.get<UserItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getUserDetails(id: string): Observable<UserDetailData> {
    return this.http.get<UserDetailData>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/details`);
  }

  createUser(dto: UserCreateDto): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.post<UserItem>(this.baseUrl, dto);
  }

  updateUser(id: string, dto: UserUpdateDto): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.put<UserItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, dto);
  }

  updateUserStatus(id: string, status: string): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.patch<UserItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/status`, { status });
  }

  resetPassword(id: string, newPassword: string): Observable<{ message: string }> {
    return this.http.post<{ message: string }>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/reset-password`, {
      new_password: newPassword,
    });
  }

  deleteUser(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getUserGroups(id: string): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/groups`);
  }

  updateUserGroups(id: string, groupIds: string[]): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.put<any[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/groups`, { group_ids: groupIds });
  }

  getUserDirectPermissions(id: string): Observable<DirectPermissionRule[]> {
    return this.http.get<DirectPermissionRule[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/permissions`);
  }

  bulkUpdateUserPermissions(
    id: string,
    permissions: { resource_type: string; resource_id?: string | null; action: string; effect: string }[]
  ): Observable<DirectPermissionRule[]> {
    return this.http.post<DirectPermissionRule[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/permissions/bulk`, {
      permissions,
    });
  }

  deleteUserPermissionRule(userId: string, ruleId: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${userId}/permissions/${ruleId}`);
  }

  getUserEffectivePermissions(
    id: string,
    search?: string,
    action?: string
  ): Observable<EffectivePermissionRow[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (search) params = params.set('search', search);
    if (action) params = params.set('action', action);
    return this.http.get<EffectivePermissionRow[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/effective-permissions`, {
      params,
    });
  }

  getUserActivity(id: string, limit: number = 50): Observable<UserActivityItem[]> {
    const params = new HttpParams().set('limit', limit);
    return this.http.get<UserActivityItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/activity`, { params });
  }

  getUserAssignedDatabases(userId: string): Observable<string[]> {
    return this.http.get<string[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${userId}/databases`);
  }

  updateUserAssignedDatabases(userId: string, databaseIds: string[]): Observable<string[]> {
    return this.http.put<string[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${userId}/databases`, {
      database_ids: databaseIds,
    });
  }

  getPhysicalTree(): Observable<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]> {
    return this.http.get<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>('/api/v1/tables/tree/physical');
  }

  getCategoryTree(): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree');
  }

  getAllConnections(): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>('/api/v1/connections');
  }
}
