import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { HasPermissionDirective } from '../../../core/directives/has-permission.directive';
import { UserManagementService } from '../services/user-management.service';
import { RoleManagementService } from '../services/role-management.service';
import {
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  PhysicalSchemaNode,
  RoleItem,
  UserCreateDto,
  UserItem,
  UserUpdateDto,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

export function splitFullName(fullName: string): { firstName: string; lastName: string } {
  const parts = (fullName || '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length === 0) return { firstName: '', lastName: '' };
  if (parts.length === 1) return { firstName: parts[0], lastName: '' };
  if (parts.length === 2) return { firstName: parts[0], lastName: parts[1] };
  if (parts.length === 3) {
    return { firstName: `${parts[0]} ${parts[1]}`, lastName: parts[2] };
  }
  return {
    firstName: `${parts[0]} ${parts[1]}`,
    lastName: parts.slice(2).join(' '),
  };
}

export interface UserRowViewModel {
  user: UserItem;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-user-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterModule, HasPermissionDirective],
  templateUrl: './user-management.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./user-management.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class UserManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private userService = inject(UserManagementService);
  private roleService = inject(RoleManagementService);

  // Users grid state
  rawUsers = signal<UserItem[]>([]);
  totalUsers = signal<number>(0);
  currentPage = signal<number>(1);
  pageSize = signal<number>(5);
  availablePageSizes = [5, 10, 20, 50];

  // Search & Filter Toolbar
  searchTerm = signal<string>('');
  filterStatus = signal<string>('ALL');

  // Column search filters (legacy / direct signals)
  filterFirstName = signal<string>('');
  filterLastName = signal<string>('');
  filterUsername = signal<string>('');
  filterEmail = signal<string>('');

  isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSaving = signal<boolean>(false);
  errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  successMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Available roles for selection
  allRoles = signal<RoleItem[]>([]);
  selectedRoleToAdd = signal<string>('');

  // Category & Physical Databases Tree
  categoryTree = signal<any[]>([]);
  allConnections = signal<any[]>([]);
  physicalTree = signal<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>([]);
  expandedDbNodes = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());
  expandedCategoryIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Virtual branch for uncategorized databases
  displayCategoryTree = computed(() => {
    const tree = this.categoryTree();
    const conns = this.allConnections().length > 0
      ? this.allConnections()
      : this.physicalTree();
    if (!conns || conns.length === 0) return tree;

    const assignedDbIds = new Set<string>();
    const collectAssigned = (nodes: any[]) => {
      for (const node of nodes) {
        if (node.id === 'uncategorized') continue;
        if (node.databases && Array.isArray(node.databases)) {
          for (const db of node.databases) {
            if (db?.id) assignedDbIds.add(db.id);
          }
        }
        if (node.children && Array.isArray(node.children)) {
          collectAssigned(node.children);
        }
      }
    };
    collectAssigned(tree);

    const uncategorizedDbs = conns
      .filter((c: any) => !assignedDbIds.has(c.id))
      .map((c: any) => ({
        id: c.id,
        name: c.name,
        database_name: c.database_name,
        engine_type: c.engine_type,
      }));

    if (uncategorizedDbs.length > 0) {
      const uncategorizedNode = {
        id: 'uncategorized',
        name: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡',
        description: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ ظ‡غŒع† ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹غŒ ط§ظ†طھط³ط§ط¨ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯',
        databases: uncategorizedDbs,
        children: [],
      };
      return [...tree, uncategorizedNode];
    }

    return tree;
  });

  // Sub-tabs in bottom detail panel
  activeDetailTab = signal<'databases' | 'permissions'>('databases');

  // Direct Administrative Permissions
  readonly directPermissionDefs = [
    { action: 'MANAGE_CATEGORIES', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹ط§طھ ظˆ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§', desc: 'طھط¹ط±غŒظپطŒ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ظˆ ط­ط°ظپ ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± ط¯ط±ط®طھغŒ ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹ط§طھ' },
    { action: 'MANAGE_CONNECTIONS', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', desc: 'ط§ظپط²ظˆط¯ظ† ظˆ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡' },
    { action: 'MANAGE_DB_USERS', label: 'ظ¾ط§غŒط´ ظˆ ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط§ظ† ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³', desc: 'ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط§ظ† ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ…طھطµظ„ ظˆ ظپط¹ط§ظ„/ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ظˆط±ظˆط¯ ط¢ظ†ظ‡ط§' },
    { action: 'MANAGE_USERS', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط§ظ†', desc: 'ط§ظپط²ظˆط¯ظ†طŒ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ظˆ ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ط­ط³ط§ط¨â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ' },
    { action: 'MANAGE_ROLES', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§ ظˆ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§', desc: 'طھط¹ط±غŒظپ ظˆ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ ظˆ ط§ظ†طھط³ط§ط¨ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§' },
    { action: 'MANAGE_SCHEDULES', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ع©ط§طھط§ظ„ظˆع¯', desc: 'طھظ†ط¸غŒظ… ط§ظ„ع¯ظˆظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط±ظˆظ† ظˆ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒ ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ط¨ظ„ط§ط¯ط±ظ†ع¯' },
    { action: 'VIEW_AUDIT_LOGS', label: 'ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ ظ„ط§ع¯â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ…ظ…غŒط²غŒ', desc: 'ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ طھط§ط±غŒط®ع†ظ‡ ظˆط±ظˆط¯طŒ طھط؛غŒغŒط±ط§طھ ط§ظ…ظ†غŒطھغŒ ظˆ ط±ط®ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ط§' },
    { action: 'EXPORT_REPORTS', label: 'ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´â€Œع¯غŒط±غŒ ع©ط§طھط§ظ„ظˆع¯ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', desc: 'ط®ط±ظˆط¬غŒ ط§ع©ط³ظ„ ظˆ JSON ط§ط² ظ…طھط§ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡' },
    { action: 'EDIT_DESCRIPTION', label: 'ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ظˆ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ', desc: 'ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ط§ظˆظ„/ط³طھظˆظ†â€Œظ‡ط§ ظˆ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ' },
  ];
  userDirectPermissions = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Master-Detail selection & Form state
  selectedUser = signal<UserItem | null>(null);
  isCreateMode = signal<boolean>(false);
  isEditMode = signal<boolean>(false);
  isFormEditable = computed<boolean>(() => this.isCreateMode() || this.isEditMode());

  // Form Fields
  formFirstName = signal<string>('');
  formLastName = signal<string>('');
  formEmail = signal<string>('');
  formUsername = signal<string>('');
  formPhone = signal<string>('');
  formPassword = signal<string>('');
  formSelectedRoleIds = signal<string[]>([]);
  formSelectedDatabaseIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Filtered and Paginated View Models
  filteredUsers = computed<UserRowViewModel[]>(() => {
    const list = this.rawUsers() || [];
    const search = this.searchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const status = this.filterStatus();
    const fnFilter = this.filterFirstName().trim().toLowerCase();
    const lnFilter = this.filterLastName().trim().toLowerCase();
    const unFilter = this.filterUsername().trim().toLowerCase();
    const emFilter = this.filterEmail().trim().toLowerCase();

    return list
      .map((u) => {
        const { firstName, lastName } = splitFullName(u.full_name);
        return { user: u, firstName, lastName };
      })
      .filter((row) => {
        if (status && status !== 'ALL' && row.user.status !== status) {
          return false;
        }
        if (search) {
          const matchSearch =
            (row.firstName || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.lastName || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.user.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.user.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.user.full_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
          if (!matchSearch) return false;
        }
        if (fnFilter && !(row.firstName || '').toLowerCase().includes(fnFilter)) return false;
        if (lnFilter && !(row.lastName || '').toLowerCase().includes(lnFilter)) return false;
        if (unFilter && !(row.user.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(unFilter)) return false;
        if (emFilter && !(row.user.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(emFilter)) return false;
        return true;
      });
  });

  paginatedUsers = computed<UserRowViewModel[]>(() => {
    const all = this.filteredUsers();
    const start = (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize();
    return all.slice(start, start + this.pageSize());
  });

  totalPages = computed<number>(() => {
    const count = this.filteredUsers().length;
    return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(count / this.pageSize()));
  });

  pageDisplayStart = computed<number>(() => {
    if (this.filteredUsers().length === 0) return 0;
    return (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize() + 1;
  });

  pageDisplayEnd = computed<number>(() => {
    return Math.min(this.currentPage() * this.pageSize(), this.filteredUsers().length);
  });

  readonly Math = Math;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadUsers();
    this.loadRoles();
    this.loadPhysicalTree();
  }

  loadUsers(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.userService.getUsers({ page: 1, page_size: 1000 }).subscribe({
      next: (resp) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        const items = Array.isArray(resp) ? resp : resp.items || [];
        this.rawUsers.set(items);
        this.totalUsers.set(items.length);

        // If a user was previously selected, refresh selection
        const current = this.selectedUser();
        if (current) {
          const fresh = items.find((u) => u.id === current.id);
          if (fresh) {
            this.selectUser(fresh);
          }
        } else if (items.length > 0 && !this.isCreateMode()) {
          this.selectUser(items[0]);
        }
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ظ„غŒط³طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط§ظ†.');
      },
    });
  }

  loadRoles(): void {
    this.roleService.getAllRolesList().subscribe({
      next: (roles) => this.allRoles.set(roles),
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  loadPhysicalTree(): void {
    this.userService.getCategoryTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.categoryTree.set(tree || []);
        const rootIds = new Set<string>();
        rootIds.add('uncategorized');
        const collectIds = (nodes: any[]) => {
          for (const n of nodes) {
            rootIds.add(n.id);
            if (n.children && n.children.length > 0) {
              collectIds(n.children);
            }
          }
        };
        collectIds(tree || []);
        this.expandedCategoryIds.set(rootIds);
      },
      error: () => {},
    });

    this.userService.getAllConnections().subscribe({
      next: (conns) => this.allConnections.set(conns || []),
      error: () => {},
    });

    this.userService.getPhysicalTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.physicalTree.set(tree);
        const initial = new Set<string>();
        tree.forEach((db) => initial.add(`db:${db.id}`));
        this.expandedDbNodes.set(initial);
      },
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  toggleCategory(catId: string): void {
    const current = new Set(this.expandedCategoryIds());
    if (current.has(catId)) {
      current.delete(catId);
    } else {
      current.add(catId);
    }
    this.expandedCategoryIds.set(current);
  }

  toggleDirectPermission(action: string, event: Event): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const checked = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
    const current = new Set(this.userDirectPermissions());
    if (checked) {
      current.add(action);
    } else {
      current.delete(action);
    }
    this.userDirectPermissions.set(current);
  }

  selectUser(user: UserItem): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(false);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedUser.set(user);
    this.populateFormWithUser(user);
  }

  startEditUser(user: UserItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) {
      event.stopPropagation();
    }
    this.selectUser(user);
    this.isEditMode.set(true);
  }

  enableEditMode(): void {
    if (this.selectedUser()) {
      this.isEditMode.set(true);
    }
  }

  private populateFormWithUser(user: UserItem): void {
    const { firstName, lastName } = splitFullName(user.full_name);
    this.formFirstName.set(firstName);
    this.formLastName.set(lastName);
    this.formEmail.set(user.email);
    this.formUsername.set(user.username);
    this.formPhone.set(user.phone || '');
    this.formPassword.set('');
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set([...(user.role_ids || [])]);

    // Load assigned databases for this user
    this.userService.getUserAssignedDatabases(user.id).subscribe({
      next: (dbIds) => {
        this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(new Set(dbIds));
      },
      error: () => {
        this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(new Set());
      },
    });

    // Load direct permissions for this user
    this.userService.getUserDirectPermissions(user.id).subscribe({
      next: (rules) => {
        const perms = new Set<string>();
        rules
          .filter((r) => r.resource_type === 'GLOBAL')
          .forEach((r) => perms.add(r.action));
        this.userDirectPermissions.set(perms);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.userDirectPermissions.set(new Set());
      },
    });
  }

  startCreateNewUser(): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(true);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedUser.set(null);
    this.formFirstName.set('');
    this.formLastName.set('');
    this.formEmail.set('');
    this.formUsername.set('');
    this.formPhone.set('');
    this.formPassword.set('');
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set([]);
    this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(new Set());
    this.userDirectPermissions.set(new Set());
    this.activeDetailTab.set('databases');
  }

  generateRandomPassword(): void {
    const chars = 'abcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789!@#$%';
    let pwd = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      pwd += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
    }
    this.formPassword.set(pwd);
  }

  cancelDetailEdit(): void {
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      this.isCreateMode.set(false);
      if (this.rawUsers().length > 0) {
        this.selectUser(this.rawUsers()[0]);
      } else {
        this.selectedUser.set(null);
      }
    } else if (this.selectedUser()) {
      this.populateFormWithUser(this.selectedUser()!);
    }
  }

  onFilterChanged(): void {
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  goToPage(p: number): void {
    if (p >= 1 && p <= this.totalPages()) {
      this.currentPage.set(p);
    }
  }

  changePageSize(newSize: number): void {
    this.pageSize.set(newSize);
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  // --- Role Management in Form ---
  addRoleToUser(roleId: string): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable() || !roleId) return;
    const current = new Set(this.formSelectedRoleIds());
    current.add(roleId);
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set(Array.from(current));
    this.selectedRoleToAdd.set('');
  }

  removeRoleFromUser(roleId: string): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const updated = this.formSelectedRoleIds().filter((id) => id !== roleId);
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set(updated);
  }

  getRoleName(roleId: string): string {
    const role = this.allRoles().find((r) => r.id === roleId);
    return role ? (role.description || role.name) : roleId;
  }

  getAvailableRolesToAdd(): RoleItem[] {
    const assigned = new Set(this.formSelectedRoleIds());
    return this.allRoles().filter((r) => !assigned.has(r.id));
  }

  // --- Database Tree Toggle & Checkbox Actions ---
  toggleNodeExpanded(key: string): void {
    const current = new Set(this.expandedDbNodes());
    if (current.has(key)) {
      current.delete(key);
    } else {
      current.add(key);
    }
    this.expandedDbNodes.set(current);
  }

  isNodeExpanded(key: string): boolean {
    return this.expandedDbNodes().has(key);
  }

  isDatabaseSelected(dbId: string): boolean {
    return this.formSelectedDatabaseIds().has(dbId);
  }

  toggleDatabaseSelection(dbId: string, event: Event): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const checked = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
    const updated = new Set(this.formSelectedDatabaseIds());
    if (checked) {
      updated.add(dbId);
    } else {
      updated.delete(dbId);
    }
    this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(updated);
  }

  getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat: any): string[] {
    const ids: string[] = [];
    const collect = (node: any) => {
      if (!node) return;
      if (node.databases && Array.isArray(node.databases)) {
        for (const db of node.databases) {
          if (db && db.id) {
            ids.push(db.id);
          }
        }
      }
      if (node.children && Array.isArray(node.children)) {
        for (const child of node.children) {
          collect(child);
        }
      }
    };
    collect(cat);
    return ids;
  }

  isCategoryChecked(cat: any): boolean {
    const dbIds = this.getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat);
    if (dbIds.length === 0) return false;
    const selected = this.formSelectedDatabaseIds();
    return dbIds.every((id) => selected.has(id));
  }

  isCategoryIndeterminate(cat: any): boolean {
    const dbIds = this.getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat);
    if (dbIds.length === 0) return false;
    const selected = this.formSelectedDatabaseIds();
    let selectedCount = 0;
    for (const id of dbIds) {
      if (selected.has(id)) {
        selectedCount++;
      }
    }
    return selectedCount > 0 && selectedCount < dbIds.length;
  }

  toggleCategorySelection(cat: any, event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const target = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const checked = target.checked;
    const dbIds = this.getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat);
    if (dbIds.length === 0) return;

    const updated = new Set(this.formSelectedDatabaseIds());
    if (checked) {
      dbIds.forEach((id) => updated.add(id));
    } else {
      dbIds.forEach((id) => updated.delete(id));
    }
    this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(updated);
  }

  // --- Save / Submit Form ---
  saveUser(): void {
    const firstName = this.formFirstName().trim();
    const lastName = this.formLastName().trim();
    const username = this.formUsername().trim();
    const email = this.formEmail().trim();
    const fullName = `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim() || firstName || lastName || username;

    if (!fullName) {
      this.showError('ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ظ†ط§ظ… غŒط§ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.');
      return;
    }

    if (!email) {
      this.showError('ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.');
      return;
    }

    if (!username) {
      this.showError('ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.');
      return;
    }

    if (username.length < 3) {
      this.showError('ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ ط´ط§ظ…ظ„ غ³ ع©ط§ط±ط§ع©طھط± ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.');
      return;
    }

    this.isSaving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    const cleanPhone = this.formPhone().trim() ? this.formPhone().trim() : undefined;

    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      if (!this.formPassword() || this.formPassword().length < 8) {
        this.isSaving.set(false);
        this.showError('ع©ظ„ظ…ظ‡ ط¹ط¨ظˆط± ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ ط´ط§ظ…ظ„ غ¸ ع©ط§ط±ط§ع©طھط± ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.');
        return;
      }

      const createDto: UserCreateDto = {
        username,
        email,
        full_name: fullName,
        phone: cleanPhone,
        password: this.formPassword(),
        role_ids: this.formSelectedRoleIds(),
      };

      this.userService.createUser(createDto).subscribe({
        next: (created) => {
          // Assign databases
          const dbIds = Array.from(this.formSelectedDatabaseIds());
          this.userService.updateUserAssignedDatabases(created.id, dbIds).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              // Assign direct permissions
              const permPayload = Array.from(this.userDirectPermissions()).map((act) => ({
                resource_type: 'GLOBAL',
                resource_id: null,
                action: act,
                effect: 'ALLOW',
              }));
              this.userService.bulkUpdateUserPermissions(created.id, permPayload).subscribe({
                next: () => {
                  this.isSaving.set(false);
                  this.showSuccess(`ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${created.full_name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ طھط¹ط±غŒظپ ظˆ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§ ط«ط¨طھ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯.`);
                  this.isCreateMode.set(false);
                  this.currentPage.set(1);
                  this.loadUsers();
                  this.selectUser(created);
                },
                error: (err) => {
                  this.isSaving.set(false);

                  this.isCreateMode.set(false);
                  this.currentPage.set(1);
                  this.loadUsers();
                  this.selectUser(created);
                },
              });
            },
            error: (err) => {
              this.isSaving.set(false);
              this.showError(`ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${created.full_name}آ» طھط¹ط±غŒظپ ط´ط¯طŒ ط§ظ…ط§ ط¯ط± طھط®طµغŒطµ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³ ط®ط·ط§غŒغŒ ط±ط® ط¯ط§ط¯: ${err?.error?.detail || err?.message || ''}`);
              this.isCreateMode.set(false);
              this.currentPage.set(1);
              this.loadUsers();
              this.selectUser(created);
            },
          });
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isSaving.set(false);
          this.showError(err?.error?.detail || err?.message || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط«ط¨طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط¬ط¯غŒط¯.');
        },
      });

        email,
        phone: this.formPhone().trim() ? this.formPhone().trim() : '',
        role_ids: this.formSelectedRoleIds(),
      };

                resource_type: 'GLOBAL',
                resource_id: null,

                      next: () => {
                        this.isSaving.set(false);
                        this.isEditMode.set(false);
                        this.showSuccess(`ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھطŒ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§ ظˆ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${updated.full_name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯.`);
                        this.loadUsers();
                      },
                      error: (err) => {
                        this.isSaving.set(false);
                        this.isEditMode.set(false);
                        this.showError(`ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯ ط§ظ…ط§ ط¨ط§ط²ظ†ط´ط§ظ†غŒ ط±ظ…ط² ط¹ط¨ظˆط± ط¨ط§ ط®ط·ط§ ظ…ظˆط§ط¬ظ‡ ط´ط¯: ${err?.error?.detail || err?.message || ''}`);

                    this.isEditMode.set(false);

        },
      });
    }
  }

  // --- Row Actions: Delete & Status ---
  toggleUserStatus(user: UserItem, event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    const nextStatus = user.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'SUSPENDED' : 'ACTIVE';
    this.userService.updateUserStatus(user.id, nextStatus).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.showSuccess(`ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${user.full_name}آ» ط¨ظ‡ ${nextStatus === 'ACTIVE' ? 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„' : 'ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ‚'} طھط؛غŒغŒط± غŒط§ظپطھ.`);
        this.loadUsers();
      },
      error: (err) => this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±.'),
    });
  }

  // Delete confirmation dialog signal
  readonly userToDelete = signal<UserItem | null>(null);

  requestDeleteUser(user: UserItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      this.showError('ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظ…ط¯غŒط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ ط³غŒط³طھظ… (SuperAdmin) ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†غŒط³طھ.');
      return;
    }
    this.userToDelete.set(user);
  }

  cancelDeleteUser(): void {
    this.userToDelete.set(null);
  }

  confirmDeleteUser(): void {
    const user = this.userToDelete();
    if (!user) return;

    this.userService.deleteUser(user.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.userToDelete.set(null);
        this.showSuccess(`ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ آ«${user.username}آ» ط­ط°ظپ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯.`);
        if (this.selectedUser()?.id === user.id) {
          this.selectedUser.set(null);
        }
        this.loadUsers();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.userToDelete.set(null);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±.');
      },
    });
  }

  deleteUser(user: UserItem, event: Event): void {
    this.requestDeleteUser(user, event);
  }

  // --- Helpers ---
  showSuccess(msg: string): void {
    this.successMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.successMessage.set(null), 4000);
  }

  showError(msg: string): void {
    this.errorMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.errorMessage.set(null), 5000);
  }
}

export interface AuditLogItem {

  created_at: string;
}

export interface AuditLogResponse {
  items: AuditLogItem[];
  page_info: KeysetPageInfo;
}

export interface AuditLogFilterParams {
  entity_type?: string;
  entity_id?: string;
  actor_id?: string;
  action?: string;
  start_time?: string;
  end_time?: string;
  cursor?: string;
  limit?: number;
}


=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\audit\services\audit.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { AuditLogFilterParams, AuditLogResponse } from '../models/audit.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class AuditService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/audit-logs';

  /**
   * Retrieves keyset-paginated immutable audit logs with multi-column filtering.
   */
  getAuditLogs(filters?: AuditLogFilterParams): Observable<AuditLogResponse> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (filters) {
      if (filters.entity_type) {
        params = params.set('entity_type', filters.entity_type);
      }
      if (filters.entity_id) {
        params = params.set('entity_id', filters.entity_id);
      }
      if (filters.actor_id) {
        params = params.set('actor_id', filters.actor_id);
      }
      if (filters.action) {
        params = params.set('action', filters.action);
      }
      if (filters.start_time) {
        params = params.set('start_time', filters.start_time);
      }
      if (filters.end_time) {
        params = params.set('end_time', filters.end_time);
      }
      if (filters.cursor) {
        params = params.set('cursor', filters.cursor);
      }
      if (filters.limit !== undefined) {
        params = params.set('limit', filters.limit.toString());
      }
    }

    return this.http.get<AuditLogResponse>(this.baseUrl, { params });
  }
}

export class LoginComponent {
  private readonly authService = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  username = "";
  password = "";
  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  onLogin(): void {
    if (!this.username || !this.password) return;
    this.executeAuth(this.username, this.password);
  }

  loginWithDemo(u: string, p: string): void {
    this.username = u;
    this.password = p;
    this.executeAuth(u, p);
  }

  private executeAuth(username: string, pass: string): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.authService
      .login({ username_or_email: username, password: pass })
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.isLoading.set(false);
          this.router.navigate(["/catalog"]);
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isLoading.set(false);
          let msg = "ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ غŒط§ ع¯ط°ط±ظˆط§عکظ‡ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ.";
          if (
            err?.status === 0 ||
            err?.status === 502 ||
            err?.status === 503 ||
            err?.status === 504
          ) {
            msg =
              "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط±ظ‚ط±ط§ط±غŒ ط§ط±طھط¨ط§ط· ط¨ط§ ط³ط±ظˆط± ط³ط±ظˆغŒط³â€Œط¯ظ‡ظ†ط¯ظ‡ (Backend). ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ط§ط² ط¯ط± ط¯ط³طھط±ط³ ط¨ظˆط¯ظ† ط³ط±ظˆط± ط§ط·ظ…غŒظ†ط§ظ† ط­ط§طµظ„ ظپط±ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.";
          } else if (err?.error?.detail) {
            msg =
              typeof err.error.detail === "string"
                ? err.error.detail
                : "ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ غŒط§ ع¯ط°ط±ظˆط§عکظ‡ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ.";
          }
          this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        },
      });
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\catalog\components\inline-desc-editor\inline-desc-editor.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  effect,
  input,
  output,
  signal,
} from "@angular/core";

import { FormsModule } from "@angular/forms";
import { TagChipComponent } from "../tag-chip/tag-chip.component";
import { TagItem } from "../../models/catalog.models";

@Component({
  selector: "app-inline-desc-editor",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, TagChipComponent],
  templateUrl: "./inline-desc-editor.component.html",
  styleUrls: ["./inline-desc-editor.component.css"],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class InlineDescEditorComponent {
  readonly description = input<string | null | undefined>(null);
  readonly placeholder = input<string>(
    "No description provided. Click to add.",
  );
  readonly canEdit = input<boolean>(true);
  readonly canManageTags = input<boolean>(false);
  readonly tags = input<TagItem[]>([]);
  readonly isLoading = input<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = input<string | null>(null);

  readonly isEditing = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly currentDescription = signal<string>("");
  readonly editValue = signal<string>("");

  readonly saved = output<string>();
  readonly cancelled = output<void>();
  readonly tagRemoved = output<TagItem>();

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      const desc = this.description();
      this.currentDescription.set(desc ?? "");
    });
  }

  startEdit(): void {
    if (!this.canEdit()) return;
    this.editValue.set(this.currentDescription());
    this.isEditing.set(true);
  }

  onInputChange(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLTextAreaElement;
    this.editValue.set(target.value);
  }

  save(): void {
    const trimmed = this.editValue().trim();
    this.currentDescription.set(trimmed);
    this.isEditing.set(false);
    this.saved.emit(trimmed);
  }

  cancelEdit(): void {
    this.editValue.set(this.currentDescription());
    this.isEditing.set(false);
    this.cancelled.emit();
  }

  onTagRemove(tag: TagItem): void {
    this.tagRemoved.emit(tag);
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\catalog\components\table-virtual-scroll\table-virtual-scroll.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  computed,
  inject,
  input,
  output,
} from "@angular/core";

import { RouterLink } from "@angular/router";
import { ScrollingModule } from "@angular/cdk/scrolling";
import { CatalogStore } from "../../stores/catalog.store";
import { SearchHit } from "../../models/catalog.models";

@Component({
  selector: "app-table-virtual-scroll",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ScrollingModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: "./table-virtual-scroll.component.html",
  styleUrls: ["./table-virtual-scroll.component.css"],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class TableVirtualScrollComponent {
  readonly store = inject(CatalogStore);

  /**
   * Optional custom items input. Defaults to store.searchHits() if not provided.
   */
  readonly itemsInput = input<SearchHit[] | undefined>(undefined, {
    alias: "items",
  });

  /**
   * Reactive signal resolving items to render.
   */
  readonly items = computed<SearchHit[]>(
    () => this.itemsInput() ?? this.store.searchHits(),
  );

  /**
   * Emitted when user scrolls near the end of the loaded catalog items.
   */
  readonly nearBottom = output<void>();

  /**
   * Deterministic trackBy function using entity UUID.
   */
  trackById(index: number, item: SearchHit): string {
    return item.id;
  }

  /**
   * Handles scroll position changes from cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport.
   * Automatically triggers keyset pagination when approaching the end of current dataset.
   */
  onScroll(scrolledIndex: number): void {
    const total = this.items().length;
    // Trigger when user is within 15 items of current end
    if (total > 0 && scrolledIndex + 15 >= total) {
      if (this.store.hasMore() && !this.store.isLoading()) {
        this.store.loadNextPage();
        this.nearBottom.emit();
      }
    }
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  input,
  output,
} from "@angular/core";

import { TagItem } from "../../models/catalog.models";

@Component({
  selector: "app-tag-chip",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [],
  templateUrl: "./tag-chip.component.html",
  styleUrls: ["./tag-chip.component.css"],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class TagChipComponent {
  readonly tag = input.required<TagItem>();
  readonly removable = input<boolean>(false);

  readonly removed = output<TagItem>();

  onRemove(event: MouseEvent): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.removed.emit(this.tag());
  }
}

import { Injectable, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { firstValueFrom } from 'rxjs';
import {
  BulkTagRequest,
  CatalogObjectsResponse,
  CategoryTreeNode,
  ChangeLogItem,
  ColumnItem,
  KeysetPageInfo,
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  SearchHit,
  SearchResponse,
  TableDetail,
  TableItem,
} from '../models/catalog.models';

export interface CatalogFilterOptions {
  database?: string;
  schema?: string;
  classification?: string;
  status?: string;
  tag?: string;
  category?: string;
  missing_descriptions_only?: boolean;
  sort_by?: string;
  min_rows?: number;
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class CatalogStore {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  // Private state signals
  private readonly _tables = signal<TableItem[]>([]);
  private readonly _searchHits = signal<SearchHit[]>([]);
  private readonly _pageInfo = signal<KeysetPageInfo | null>(null);
  private readonly _isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  private readonly _activeFilter = signal<string>('');
  private readonly _activeFilterOptions = signal<CatalogFilterOptions>({});
  private readonly _error = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Public readonly selectors
  readonly tables = this._tables.asReadonly();
  readonly searchHits = this._searchHits.asReadonly();
  readonly isLoading = this._isLoading.asReadonly();
  readonly pageInfo = this._pageInfo.asReadonly();
  readonly activeFilter = this._activeFilter.asReadonly();
  readonly activeFilterOptions = this._activeFilterOptions.asReadonly();
  readonly error = this._error.asReadonly();

  // Computed signals
  readonly hasMore = computed<boolean>(() => this._pageInfo()?.has_next_page ?? false);
  readonly totalHitCount = computed<number>(() => this._searchHits().length);

  /**
   * Executes keyset-paginated search against the catalog API.
   * If reset is true, clears accumulated results and starts from beginning.
   */
  async search(
    query: string = '',
    reset: boolean = true,
    filters?: CatalogFilterOptions
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (reset) {
      this._searchHits.set([]);
      this._pageInfo.set(null);
      this._activeFilter.set(query);
      if (filters !== undefined) {
        this._activeFilterOptions.set(filters);
      }
    }

    this._isLoading.set(true);
    this._error.set(null);

    const activeOpts = filters !== undefined ? filters : this._activeFilterOptions();
    const cursor = reset ? '' : (this._pageInfo()?.next_cursor || '');
    let url = `/api/v1/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=50&cursor=${cursor}`;

    if (activeOpts.database) {
      url += `&database=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.database)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.schema) {
      url += `&schema=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.schema)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.classification) {
      url += `&classification=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.classification)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.status) {
      url += `&status=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.status)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.tag) {
      url += `&tag=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.tag)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.category) {
      url += `&category=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.category)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.missing_descriptions_only) {
      url += `&missing_descriptions_only=true`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.sort_by) {
      url += `&sort_by=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.sort_by)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.min_rows !== undefined && activeOpts.min_rows !== null) {
      url += `&min_rows=${activeOpts.min_rows}`;
    }

    try {
      const resp = await firstValueFrom(this.http.get<SearchResponse>(url));
      this._searchHits.update(current => (reset ? resp.items : [...current, ...resp.items]));
      this._pageInfo.set(resp.page_info);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Catalog search request failed';
      this._error.set(msg);
      throw err;
    } finally {
      this._isLoading.set(false);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Appends the next keyset page to the existing search results.
   */
  async loadNextPage(): Promise<void> {
    if (this._isLoading() || !this.hasMore()) {
      return;
    }
    const currentQuery = this._activeFilter();
    await this.search(currentQuery, false);
  }

  /**
   * Updates approved description for a table and synchronizes reactive state.
   */
  async updateDescription(tableId: string, approvedDescription: string): Promise<void> {
    const updated = await firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<{ id: string; effective_description: string }>(
        `/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/description`,
        { approved_description: approvedDescription }
      )
    );

    const newDesc = updated.effective_description;

    this._tables.update(list =>
      list.map(t => (t.id === tableId ? { ...t, effective_description: newDesc } : t))
    );

    this._searchHits.update(list =>
      list.map(hit => (hit.id === tableId ? { ...hit, effective_description: newDesc } : hit))
    );
  }

  /**
   * Updates owner for a table and synchronizes reactive state.
   */
  async updateTableOwner(tableId: string, owner: string): Promise<TableDetail> {
    const updated = await firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<TableDetail>(
        `/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/owner`,
        { owner }
      )
    );

    this._tables.update(list =>
      list.map(t => (t.id === tableId ? { ...t, owner: updated.owner } : t))
    );

    return updated;
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves full table details including columns and tags.
   */
  async getTableDetails(tableId: string): Promise<TableDetail> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<TableDetail>(`/api/v1/tables/${tableId}`));
  }

  /**
   * Approves AI-suggested description for a table.
   */
  async approveAiTableDescription(tableId: string): Promise<TableDetail> {
    const updated = await firstValueFrom(
      this.http.post<TableDetail>(`/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/approve-ai-description`, {})
    );
    this._tables.update((list) =>
      list.map((t) => (t.id === tableId ? { ...t, effective_description: updated.effective_description } : t))
    );
    this._searchHits.update((list) =>
      list.map((hit) => (hit.id === tableId ? { ...hit, effective_description: updated.effective_description } : hit))
    );
    return updated;
  }

  /**
   * Approves AI-suggested description for a column.
   */
  async approveAiColumnDescription(columnId: string): Promise<ColumnItem> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.post<ColumnItem>(`/api/v1/columns/${columnId}/approve-ai-description`, {})
    );
  }

  /**
   * Updates approved description for a column.
   */
  async updateColumnDescription(columnId: string, approvedDescription: string): Promise<ColumnItem> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<ColumnItem>(`/api/v1/columns/${columnId}/description`, {
        approved_description: approvedDescription,
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Updates security classification for a column.
   */
  async updateColumnClassification(columnId: string, classificationLevel: string): Promise<ColumnItem> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<ColumnItem>(`/api/v1/columns/${columnId}/classification`, {
        classification_level: classificationLevel,
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Fetches change log history for a table.
   */
  async getTableHistory(tableId: string): Promise<ChangeLogItem[]> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<ChangeLogItem[]>(`/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/change-logs`));
  }

  /**
   * Fetches physical database tree with optional advanced search and filtering.
   */
  async getPhysicalTree(filters?: {
    search?: string;
    engine_type?: string;
    host?: string;
    owner?: string;
    is_active?: boolean;
  }): Promise<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (filters?.search) params = params.set('search', filters.search);
    if (filters?.engine_type) params = params.set('engine_type', filters.engine_type);
    if (filters?.host) params = params.set('host', filters.host);
    if (filters?.owner) params = params.set('owner', filters.owner);
    if (filters?.is_active !== undefined) params = params.set('is_active', String(filters.is_active));
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>('/api/v1/tables/tree/physical', { params }));
  }

  /**
   * Fetches dedicated catalog objects (Tables, Views, Indexes, Procedures, Functions) for a given scope.
   */
  async getCatalogObjects(scope?: {
    database?: string;
    schema?: string;
    table_id?: string;
    object_type?: string;
  }): Promise<CatalogObjectsResponse> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (scope?.database) params = params.set('database', scope.database);
    if (scope?.schema) params = params.set('schema', scope.schema);
    if (scope?.table_id) params = params.set('table_id', scope.table_id);
    if (scope?.object_type) params = params.set('object_type', scope.object_type);
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<CatalogObjectsResponse>('/api/v1/catalog/objects', { params }));
  }

  /**
   * Fetches category business hierarchy tree.
   */
  async getCategoryTree(): Promise<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree'));
  }

  async loadCategoryTree(): Promise<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return this.getCategoryTree();
  }

  /**
   * Executes bulk tag assignment across entities.
   */
  async bulkTag(request: BulkTagRequest): Promise<{ success: boolean; affected_count: number }> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.post<{ success: boolean; affected_count: number }>('/api/v1/tags/bulk-assign', request)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Sets searchHits directly (useful for testing and component state priming).
   */
  setSearchHits(hits: SearchHit[]): void {
    this._searchHits.set(hits);
  }

  /**
   * Sets tables directly.
   */
  setTables(tables: TableItem[]): void {
    this._tables.set(tables);
  }

  /**
   * Resets all store state to initial defaults.
   */
  reset(): void {
    this._tables.set([]);
    this._searchHits.set([]);
    this._pageInfo.set(null);
    this._isLoading.set(false);
    this._activeFilter.set('');
    this._activeFilterOptions.set({});
    this._error.set(null);
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { ScrollingModule } from '@angular/cdk/scrolling';
import { CatalogStore } from './stores/catalog.store';
import {
  CatalogObjectItem,
  CatalogObjectsResponse,
  CategoryTreeNode,
  ChangeLogItem,
  ColumnItem,
  DescriptionPolicy,
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  SearchHit,
  TableDetail,
} from './models/catalog.models';
import { InlineDescEditorComponent } from './components/inline-desc-editor/inline-desc-editor.component';
import { DriftBannerComponent } from '../drift/drift-banner.component';
import { DriftResolutionDialogComponent } from '../drift/drift-resolution-dialog.component';
import { TableStatisticsChartComponent } from './table-statistics-chart.component';
import { HasPermissionDirective } from '../../core/directives/has-permission.directive';
import { RbacService } from '../../core/auth/rbac.service';
import { SyncJobService } from '../../core/services/sync-job.service';
import { ActivatedRoute } from '@angular/router';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-catalog-explorer',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    FormsModule,
    ScrollingModule,
    InlineDescEditorComponent,
    DriftBannerComponent,
    DriftResolutionDialogComponent,
    TableStatisticsChartComponent,
    HasPermissionDirective,
  ],
  templateUrl: './catalog-explorer.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./catalog-explorer.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class CatalogExplorerComponent implements OnInit {
  readonly store = inject(CatalogStore);
  readonly rbac = inject(RbacService);
  readonly syncJobService = inject(SyncJobService);
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute, { optional: true });

  // Description Display Policy: 'APPROVED_FIRST' | 'SOURCE_FIRST' | 'BOTH'
  readonly descPolicy = signal<DescriptionPolicy>('BOTH');

  // Search & Filter State
  searchQuery = '';
  selectedDatabase = '';
  selectedSchema = '';
  selectedClassification = '';
  selectedStatus = '';
  selectedCategory = '';
  missingDescriptionsOnly = signal<boolean>(false);
  selectedSortBy = signal<string>('row_count_desc');
  hideLowRowTables = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Advanced Tree Filtering State
  readonly dbSearchFilter = signal<string>('');
  readonly dbHostFilter = signal<string>('');
  readonly dbEngineFilter = signal<string>('');
  readonly dbOwnerFilter = signal<string>('');

  // Trees State
  activeTreeTab = signal<'physical' | 'category'>('physical');
  physicalTree = signal<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>([]);
  categoryTree = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  isTreeLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSidebarExpanded = signal<boolean>(true);

  // Filtered physical database tree based on search & filter criteria
  readonly filteredPhysicalTree = computed<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>(() => {
    const term = this.dbSearchFilter().trim().toLowerCase();
    const hostTerm = this.dbHostFilter().trim().toLowerCase();
    const engineTerm = this.dbEngineFilter().trim().toUpperCase();
    const ownerTerm = this.dbOwnerFilter().trim().toLowerCase();

    return this.physicalTree().filter((conn) => {
      if (term) {
        const matchesName = (conn.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(term);
        const matchesDb = (conn.database_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(term);
        if (!matchesName && !matchesDb) return false;
      }
      if (hostTerm) {
        if (!conn.host || !conn.host.toLowerCase().includes(hostTerm)) return false;
      }
      if (engineTerm) {
        if (!conn.engine_type || conn.engine_type.toUpperCase() !== engineTerm) return false;
      }
      if (ownerTerm) {
        if (!conn.owner || !conn.owner.toLowerCase().includes(ownerTerm)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  });

  clearDbFilters(): void {
    this.dbSearchFilter.set('');
    this.dbHostFilter.set('');
    this.dbEngineFilter.set('');
    this.dbOwnerFilter.set('');
  }

  // 5 Dedicated Object Tabs State
  readonly activeObjectTab = signal<'tables' | 'views' | 'indexes' | 'procedures' | 'functions'>('tables');
  readonly catalogObjects = signal<CatalogObjectsResponse | null>(null);
  readonly isLoadingObjects = signal<boolean>(false);

  readonly objectCounts = computed(() => {
    const objs = this.catalogObjects();
    return {
      tables: objs?.counts?.tables ?? this.store.searchHits().length,
      views: objs?.counts?.views ?? 0,
      indexes: objs?.counts?.indexes ?? 0,
      procedures: objs?.counts?.procedures ?? 0,
      functions: objs?.counts?.functions ?? 0,
    };
  });

  // Multi-selection for bulk tagging
  selectedTableIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Table Detail Inspector Drawer
  selectedTable = signal<TableDetail | null>(null);
  selectedTableId = signal<string | null>(null);
  activeInspectorTab = signal<'columns' | 'metadata' | 'indexes' | 'history' | 'statistics'>('columns');
  isInspectorLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  tableHistory = signal<ChangeLogItem[]>([]);
  showDriftResolution = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Table Inspector Indexes (filtered specifically for selectedTable().id)
  readonly tableSpecificIndexes = signal<CatalogObjectItem[] | null>(null);

  readonly tableIndexes = computed<CatalogObjectItem[]>(() => {
    const tbl = this.selectedTable();
    if (!tbl) return [];

    const specific = this.tableSpecificIndexes();
    if (specific !== null) {
      return specific;
    }

    const allIndexes = this.catalogObjects()?.indexes || [];
    return allIndexes.filter((idx) => {
      const idxTableId = idx.details?.['table_id'];
      if (idxTableId) {
        return idxTableId === tbl.id;
      }
      return idx.table_name === tbl.name;
    });
  });

  openDriftResolution(): void {
    this.showDriftResolution.set(true);
  }

  onDriftResolved(): void {
    const tid = this.selectedTableId();
    if (tid) {
      this.inspectTable(tid);
    }
  }

  // Toast Notification
  toastMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  toastType = signal<'success' | 'error' | 'info'>('success');

  // Inline Owner Editor
  isEditingOwner = signal<boolean>(false);
  editOwnerValue = signal<string>('');

  // Computed Selection
  hasSelectedTables = computed(() => this.selectedTableIds().size > 0);
  selectedCount = computed(() => this.selectedTableIds().size);

  // 4-Card Scope KPI Strip (Card 1 dynamically reflects active object tab)
  readonly activeScopeKpi = computed(() => {
    const tab = this.activeObjectTab();
    const objs = this.catalogObjects();
    switch (tab) {
      case 'views':
        return {
          label: 'طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ظ„ ظ†ظ…ط§ظ‡ط§',
          count: objs?.views?.length ?? this.objectCounts().views,
          icon: 'pi pi-eye',
        };
      case 'indexes':
        return {
          label: 'طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ظ„ ط§غŒظ†ط¯ع©ط³â€Œظ‡ط§',
          count: objs?.indexes?.length ?? this.objectCounts().indexes,
          icon: 'pi pi-bolt',
        };
      case 'procedures':
        return {
          label: 'طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ظ„ ط±ظˆط§ظ„â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡',
          count: objs?.procedures?.length ?? this.objectCounts().procedures,
          icon: 'pi pi-cog',
        };
      case 'functions':
        return {
          label: 'طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ظ„ طھظˆط§ط¨ط¹',
          count: objs?.functions?.length ?? this.objectCounts().functions,
          icon: 'pi pi-code',
        };
      case 'tables':
      default:
        return {
          label: 'طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ظ„ ط¬ط¯ط§ظˆظ„',
          count: this.store.searchHits().length || this.objectCounts().tables,
          icon: 'pi pi-table',
        };
    }
  });

  readonly kpiDynamicLabel = computed(() => this.activeScopeKpi().label);
  readonly kpiDynamicCount = computed(() => this.activeScopeKpi().count);
  readonly kpiDynamicIcon = computed(() => this.activeScopeKpi().icon);
  readonly kpiTotalTables = computed(() => this.store.searchHits().length);

  readonly kpiDocumentedPct = computed(() => {
    const hits = this.store.searchHits();
    if (hits.length === 0) return 0;
    const docCount = hits.filter(
      (h) => Boolean(h.approved_description?.trim() || h.source_description?.trim() || h.effective_description?.trim())
    ).length;
    return Math.round((docCount / hits.length) * 100);
  });

  readonly kpiSensitiveColumnsCount = computed(() => {
    const hits = this.store.searchHits();
    return hits.filter(
      (h) =>
        h.classification_level === 'PII' ||
        h.classification_level === 'CONFIDENTIAL' ||
        h.tags?.some((t) => {
          const lower = (t.name || '').toLowerCase();
          return lower.includes('pii') || lower.includes('confidential') || lower.includes('ظ…ط­ط±ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‡');
        })
    ).length;
  });

  readonly kpiDriftCount = computed(() => {
    return this.store.searchHits().filter((h) => Boolean(h.description_drift_detected)).length;
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    // 0. Load persisted description display policy
    try {
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        const savedPolicy = localStorage.getItem('datacatalog_desc_policy') as DescriptionPolicy;
        if (savedPolicy === 'APPROVED_FIRST' || savedPolicy === 'SOURCE_FIRST' || savedPolicy === 'BOTH') {
          this.descPolicy.set(savedPolicy);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore in non-browser/restricted environments
    }

    // 1. Initial Catalog Load
    this.executeSearch();

    // 2. Query Params Sync (e.g. from top global search bar)
    if (this.route) {
      this.route.queryParams.subscribe((params) => {
        const q = params['q'];
        if (q !== undefined && q !== this.searchQuery) {
          this.searchQuery = q;
          this.executeSearch();
        }
      });
    }

    // 3. Load Hierarchy Trees
    this.loadTrees();

    // 4. Load Scope Catalog Objects
    this.loadCatalogObjects();
  }

  async loadCatalogObjects(): Promise<void> {
    this.isLoadingObjects.set(true);
    try {
      const res = await this.store.getCatalogObjects({
        database: this.selectedDatabase || undefined,
        schema: this.selectedSchema || undefined,
      });
      this.catalogObjects.set(res);
    } catch {
      // Handled gracefully
    } finally {
      this.isLoadingObjects.set(false);
    }
  }

  setActiveObjectTab(tab: 'tables' | 'views' | 'indexes' | 'procedures' | 'functions'): void {
    this.activeObjectTab.set(tab);
  }

  setDescPolicy(policy: DescriptionPolicy): void {
    this.descPolicy.set(policy);
    try {
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem('datacatalog_desc_policy', policy);
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  triggerDatabaseSync(conn: PhysicalDatabaseNode, event: MouseEvent): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.syncJobService.triggerSync(conn.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.showToast(`ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ظ…طھط§ط¯غŒطھط§ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ آ«${conn.name}آ» ط¢ط؛ط§ط² ط´ط¯.`, 'success');
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : `ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¢ط؛ط§ط² ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ آ«${conn.name}آ»`;
        this.showToast(msg, 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  navigateToAll(): void {
    this.selectedDatabase = '';
    this.selectedSchema = '';
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  navigateToDatabase(dbName: string): void {
    this.selectedDatabase = dbName;
    this.selectedSchema = '';
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  getDisplayDescription(hit: SearchHit): {
    policy: DescriptionPolicy;
    primaryText: string;
    origin: 'approved' | 'source' | 'none';
    secondaryText?: string;
    secondaryOrigin?: 'approved' | 'source';
  } {
    const policy = this.descPolicy();
    const app = hit.approved_description?.trim();
    const src = hit.source_description?.trim();
    const eff = hit.effective_description?.trim();

    if (policy === 'APPROVED_FIRST') {
      if (app) {
        return { policy, primaryText: app, origin: 'approved' };
      }
      if (src) {
        return { policy, primaryText: src, origin: 'source' };
      }
      if (eff) {
        return { policy, primaryText: eff, origin: 'source' };
      }
      return { policy, primaryText: 'ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ظ‡', origin: 'none' };
    }

    if (policy === 'SOURCE_FIRST') {
      if (src) {
        return { policy, primaryText: src, origin: 'source' };
      }
      if (app) {
        return { policy, primaryText: app, origin: 'approved' };
      }
      if (eff) {
        return { policy, primaryText: eff, origin: 'source' };
      }
      return { policy, primaryText: 'ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ظ‡', origin: 'none' };
    }

    // BOTH
    if (app && src) {
      return {
        policy,
        primaryText: app,
        origin: 'approved',
        secondaryText: src,
        secondaryOrigin: 'source',
      };
    }
    if (app) {
      return { policy, primaryText: app, origin: 'approved' };
    }
    if (src) {
      return { policy, primaryText: src, origin: 'source' };
    }
    if (eff) {
      return { policy, primaryText: eff, origin: 'source' };
    }
    return { policy, primaryText: 'ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ظ‡', origin: 'none' };
  }

  executeSearch(): void {
    const filters = {
      database: this.selectedDatabase || undefined,
      schema: this.selectedSchema || undefined,
      classification: this.selectedClassification || undefined,
      status: this.selectedStatus || undefined,
      category: this.selectedCategory || undefined,
      missing_descriptions_only: this.missingDescriptionsOnly() || undefined,
      sort_by: this.selectedSortBy(),
      min_rows: this.hideLowRowTables() ? 10 : undefined,
    };
    this.store.search(this.searchQuery, true, filters).catch(() => {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط±ظ‚ط±ط§ط±غŒ ط§ط±طھط¨ط§ط· ط¨ط§ ط³ط±ظˆط± ظ…طھط§ط¯غŒطھط§', 'error');
    });
    this.loadCatalogObjects();
  }

  toggleMissingDescriptionsOnly(): void {
    this.missingDescriptionsOnly.update((v) => !v);
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  toggleHideLowRowTables(): void {
    this.hideLowRowTables.update((v) => !v);
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  onSortChange(sortBy: string): void {
    this.selectedSortBy.set(sortBy);
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  isTableEmpty(hit: SearchHit): boolean {
    return (hit.row_count_estimate || 0) === 0;
  }

  isTableLowRow(hit: SearchHit): boolean {
    const c = hit.row_count_estimate || 0;
    return c >= 1 && c < 10;
  }

  resetFilters(): void {
    this.searchQuery = '';
    this.selectedDatabase = '';
    this.selectedSchema = '';
    this.selectedClassification = '';
    this.selectedStatus = '';
    this.selectedCategory = '';
    this.missingDescriptionsOnly.set(false);
    this.hideLowRowTables.set(false);
    this.selectedSortBy.set('row_count_desc');
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  clearSelection(): void {
    this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set());
  }

  async loadTrees(): Promise<void> {
    this.isTreeLoading.set(true);
    try {
      const [phys, cat] = await Promise.all([
        this.store.getPhysicalTree(),
        this.store.getCategoryTree(),
      ]);
      this.physicalTree.set(phys);
      this.categoryTree.set(cat);
    } catch {
      // Ignored or handled gracefully
    } finally {
      this.isTreeLoading.set(false);
    }
  }

  // Tree Node Selection Handlers
  filterByDatabase(databaseName: string): void {
    this.selectedDatabase = databaseName;
    this.selectedSchema = '';
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  filterBySchema(schemaName: string): void {
    this.selectedSchema = schemaName;
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  filterByCategory(categoryIdentifier: string): void {
    this.selectedCategory = categoryIdentifier;
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  filterByTable(tableName: string): void {
    this.searchQuery = tableName;
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  // Selection Checkbox Handlers
  toggleTableSelection(id: string): void {
    this.selectedTableIds.update((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  toggleSelectAll(): void {
    const hits = this.store.searchHits();
    const current = this.selectedTableIds();
    if (current.size === hits.length && hits.length > 0) {
      this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set());
    } else {
      this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set(hits.map((h) => h.id)));
    }
  }

  isTableSelected(id: string): boolean {
    return this.selectedTableIds().has(id);
  }

  // Table Detail Inspector Drawer
  async inspectTable(tableId: string): Promise<void> {
    this.isEditingOwner.set(false);
    this.selectedTableId.set(tableId);
    this.isInspectorLoading.set(true);
    this.tableSpecificIndexes.set(null);
    try {
      const [detail, history, objRes] = await Promise.all([
        this.store.getTableDetails(tableId),
        this.store.getTableHistory(tableId),
        this.store.getCatalogObjects({ table_id: tableId }).catch(() => null),
      ]);
      this.selectedTable.set(detail);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      if (objRes && Array.isArray(objRes.indexes)) {
        this.tableSpecificIndexes.set(objRes.indexes);
      }
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط¹ط¯ظ… ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ غŒط§ ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط´ظ†ط§ط³ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„', 'error');
      this.selectedTable.set(null);
    } finally {
      this.isInspectorLoading.set(false);
    }
  }

  closeInspector(): void {
    this.isEditingOwner.set(false);
    this.selectedTable.set(null);
    this.selectedTableId.set(null);
    this.tableSpecificIndexes.set(null);
  }

  // AI Suggestion Approvals
  async approveAiTableDescription(tableId: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.approveAiTableDescription(tableId);
      this.selectedTable.set(updated);
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(tableId);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast('ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظˆ ط¨ظ‡ ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ط´ط±ط­ ظ†ظ‡ط§غŒغŒ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯', 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ', 'error');
    }
  }

  async approveAiColumnDescription(col: ColumnItem): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.approveAiColumnDescription(col.id);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => {
        if (!curr) return null;
        return {
          ...curr,
          columns: curr.columns.map((c) => (c.id === col.id ? updated : c)),
        };
      });
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(col.table_id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast(`ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط³طھظˆظ† آ«${col.name}آ» طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯`, 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ ط³طھظˆظ†', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Table Approved Description Editor
  async onSaveTableDescription(newDesc: string): Promise<void> {
    const tbl = this.selectedTable();
    if (!tbl) return;
    try {
      await this.store.updateDescription(tbl.id, newDesc);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => (curr ? { ...curr, approved_description: newDesc, effective_description: newDesc } : curr));
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(tbl.id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast('طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظˆ ط¯ط± ط²ظ†ط¬غŒط±ظ‡ طھط؛غŒغŒط±ط§طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯', 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Table Owner Inline Editor
  startEditingOwner(): void {
    const current = this.selectedTable()?.owner || '';
    this.editOwnerValue.set(current);
    this.isEditingOwner.set(true);
  }

  cancelEditingOwner(): void {
    this.isEditingOwner.set(false);
  }

  async saveTableOwner(): Promise<void> {
    const tbl = this.selectedTable();
    if (!tbl) return;
    const newOwner = this.editOwnerValue().trim();
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.updateTableOwner(tbl.id, newOwner);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => (curr ? { ...curr, owner: updated.owner } : curr));
      this.isEditingOwner.set(false);
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(tbl.id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast('ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظˆ ط¯ط± ط²ظ†ط¬غŒط±ظ‡ طھط؛غŒغŒط±ط§طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯', 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Column Approved Description Editor
  async onSaveColumnDescription(col: ColumnItem, newDesc: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.updateColumnDescription(col.id, newDesc);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => {
        if (!curr) return null;
        return {
          ...curr,
          columns: curr.columns.map((c) => (c.id === col.id ? updated : c)),
        };
      });
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(col.table_id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast(`طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ظپغŒظ„ط¯ ${col.name} ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯`, 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط³طھظˆظ†', 'error');
    }
  }

  isTextColumn(col: ColumnItem): boolean {
    if (!col || !col.data_type) return false;
    const dt = col.data_type.toLowerCase();
    return (
      dt.includes('char') ||
      dt.includes('text') ||
      dt.includes('string') ||
      dt.includes('clob') ||
      dt.includes('varchar')
    );
  }

  // Column Classification Dropdown Update
  async onClassificationChange(col: ColumnItem, newLevel: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.updateColumnClassification(col.id, newLevel);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => {
        if (!curr) return null;
        return {
          ...curr,
          columns: curr.columns.map((c) => (c.id === col.id ? updated : c)),
        };
      });
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(col.table_id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast(`ط³ط·ط­ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…ط§ظ†ع¯غŒ ظپغŒظ„ط¯ ${col.name} ط¨ظ‡ ${newLevel} طھط؛غŒغŒط± غŒط§ظپطھ`, 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط؛غŒغŒط± ط³ط·ط­ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…ط§ظ†ع¯غŒ', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Toast Helper
  showToast(message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info' = 'success'): void {
    this.toastMessage.set(message);
    this.toastType.set(type);
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.toastMessage.set(null);
    }, 4000);
  }

  trackByHitId(index: number, hit: SearchHit): string {
    return hit.id;
  }
}

export class TableStatisticsChartComponent implements OnChanges {
  private readonly statsService = inject(TableStatsService);

  readonly tableId = input<string | null>(null);

  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly dataPoints = signal<TableStatisticPoint[]>([]);

  private readonly persianDateFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });

  ngOnChanges(changes: SimpleChanges): void {
    const tableId = this.tableId();
    if (changes["tableId"] && tableId) {
      this.loadStatistics(tableId);
    }
  }

  loadStatistics(id: string): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.statsService.getTableStatistics(id, 30).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        // Backend returns in DESC order, reverse for chronological chart view
        const points = [...(res.data_points || [])].reverse();
        this.dataPoints.set(points);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg =
          err instanceof Error
            ? err.message
            : "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ طھط§ط±غŒط®ع†ظ‡ ط¢ظ…ط§ط± ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„";
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  readonly chartOptions = computed<echarts.EChartsCoreOption>(() => {
    const points = this.dataPoints();
    const xLabels = points.map((p) => {
      try {
        return this.persianDateFormatter.format(new Date(p.recorded_at));
      } catch {
        return p.recorded_at;
      }
    });

    const rowCounts = points.map((p) => p.row_count_estimate);
    const sizesMB = points.map((p) =>
      Number((p.size_bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)),
    );

    return {
      textStyle: {
        fontFamily:
          "Vazirmatn, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Segoe UI, Roboto, sans-serif",
      },
      tooltip: {
        trigger: "axis",
        textStyle: {
          fontFamily: "Vazirmatn, sans-serif",
        },
      },
      legend: {
        data: ["طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط±ع©ظˆط±ط¯ظ‡ط§ (Rows)", "ط­ط¬ظ… ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ (MB)"],
        top: 0,
        textStyle: {
          fontFamily: "Vazirmatn, sans-serif",
        },
      },
      grid: {
        left: "4%",
        right: "4%",
        bottom: "8%",
        top: "18%",
        containLabel: true,
      },
      xAxis: {
        type: "category",
        boundaryGap: false,
        data: xLabels,
        axisLabel: {
          fontFamily: "Vazirmatn, sans-serif",
          rotate: xLabels.length > 8 ? 30 : 0,
        },
      },
      yAxis: [
        {
          type: "value",
          name: "طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط±ع©ظˆط±ط¯ظ‡ط§ (Rows)",
          position: "right",
          axisLabel: {
            fontFamily: "Vazirmatn, sans-serif",
          },
        },
        {
          type: "value",
          name: "ط­ط¬ظ… (ظ…ع¯ط§ط¨ط§غŒطھ)",
          position: "left",
          axisLabel: {
            fontFamily: "Vazirmatn, sans-serif",
          },
        },
      ],
      series: [
        {
          name: "طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط±ع©ظˆط±ط¯ظ‡ط§ (Rows)",
          type: "line",
          yAxisIndex: 0,
          data: rowCounts,
          smooth: true,
          itemStyle: { color: "#2563eb" },
          areaStyle: {
            color: new echarts.graphic.LinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 1, [
              { offset: 0, color: "rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.25)" },
              { offset: 1, color: "rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.02)" },
            ]),
          },
        },
        {
          name: "ط­ط¬ظ… ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ (MB)",
          type: "line",
          yAxisIndex: 1,
          data: sizesMB,
          smooth: true,
          itemStyle: { color: "#16a34a" },
        },
      ],
    };
  });
}

export type DatabaseEngine = 'POSTGRESQL' | 'MSSQL' | 'ORACLE' | 'MYSQL' | 'CLICKHOUSE' | 'MONGODB';

export interface SyncSchedule {
  id: string;
  connection_id: string;
  cron_expression: string;
  is_enabled: boolean;
  circuit_state?: 'CLOSED' | 'OPEN' | 'HALF_OPEN';
  consecutive_failures?: number;
  cooldown_until?: string | null;
  last_run_at?: string | null;
  last_run_status?: string | null;
  last_run_message?: string | null;
  next_run_at?: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface DatabaseConnection {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  engine_type: DatabaseEngine;
  host: string;
  port: number;
  database_name: string;
  default_schema?: string | null;
  collation?: string | null;
  owner: string;

  category_name?: string | null;
  custom_params?: Record<string, unknown>;
  masked_credentials?: Record<string, string>;
  sync_schedule?: SyncSchedule | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface ConnectionCreateRequest {
  name: string;
  engine_type: DatabaseEngine;
  host: string;
  port: number;
  database_name: string;
  default_schema?: string;
  owner?: string;
  category_id?: string | null;
  credentials: Record<string, unknown>;
  custom_params?: Record<string, unknown>;
  cron_expression?: string;
}

  success: boolean;
  latency_ms: number;
  dialect: DatabaseEngine;
  message: string;
  diagnostic_details?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  tested_at: string;
}


=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\connections\services\connection.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  ConnectionCreateRequest,
  ConnectionTestResult,
  DatabaseConnection,
} from '../models/connection.models';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../../catalog/models/catalog.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class ConnectionService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/connections';

  /**
   * Retrieves business category hierarchy tree.
   */
  getCategoryTree(): Observable<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree');
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves all registered database connections with optional active filter.
   */
  getConnections(isActive?: boolean): Observable<DatabaseConnection[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (isActive !== undefined) {
      params = params.set('is_active', isActive.toString());
    }
    return this.http.get<DatabaseConnection[]>(this.baseUrl, { params });
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves single database connection by ID.
   */
  getConnection(id: string): Observable<DatabaseConnection> {
    return this.http.get<DatabaseConnection>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  /**
   * Registers a new database connection with envelope encrypted credentials.
   */
  createConnection(request: ConnectionCreateRequest): Observable<DatabaseConnection> {
    return this.http.post<DatabaseConnection>(this.baseUrl, request);
  }

  /**
   * Updates an existing database connection.
   */
  updateConnection(id: string, request: Partial<ConnectionCreateRequest>): Observable<DatabaseConnection> {
    return this.http.put<DatabaseConnection>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, request);
  }

  /**
   * Deletes a database connection.
   */
  deleteConnection(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  /**
   * Executes live dialect connection ping test with 5s timeout.
   */
  testConnection(id: string): Observable<ConnectionTestResult> {
    return this.http.post<ConnectionTestResult>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/test`, {});
  }

  /**
   * Auto-discovers non-template databases from host.
   */
  discoverDatabases(payload: {
    engine_type: string;
    host: string;
    port: number;
    credentials: { username: string; password?: string };
    custom_params?: any;
  }): Observable<{ success: boolean; databases: string[]; latency_ms: number; server_version?: string; message: string }> {
    return this.http.post<{ success: boolean; databases: string[]; latency_ms: number; server_version?: string; message: string }>(
      `${this.baseUrl}/discover-databases`,
      payload
    );
  }

  /**
   * Executes live diagnostic connectivity test on unsaved connection payload before saving.
   */
  testUnsavedConnection(payload: {
    engine_type: string;
    host: string;
    port: number;
    database_name: string;
    credentials: { username: string; password?: string };
    custom_params?: any;
  }): Observable<ConnectionTestResult> {
    return this.http.post<ConnectionTestResult>(`${this.baseUrl}/test-unsaved`, payload);
  }
}


=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\connections\validators\cron.validator.ts ===
import { AbstractControl, ValidationErrors, ValidatorFn } from '@angular/forms';

const MONTH_NAMES: Record<string, number> = {
  JAN: 1,
  FEB: 2,
  MAR: 3,
  APR: 4,
  MAY: 5,
  JUN: 6,
  JUL: 7,
  AUG: 8,
  SEP: 9,
  OCT: 10,
  NOV: 11,
  DEC: 12,
};

const DOW_NAMES: Record<string, number> = {
  SUN: 0,
  MON: 1,
  TUE: 2,
  WED: 3,
  THU: 4,
  FRI: 5,
  SAT: 6,
};

interface FieldBound {
  name: string;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  nameMap?: Record<string, number>;
}

const FIELD_BOUNDS: FieldBound[] = [
  { name: 'minute', min: 0, max: 59 },
  { name: 'hour', min: 0, max: 23 },
  { name: 'day of month', min: 1, max: 31 },
  { name: 'month', min: 1, max: 12, nameMap: MONTH_NAMES },
  { name: 'day of week', min: 0, max: 7, nameMap: DOW_NAMES },
];

function parseToken(token: string, nameMap?: Record<string, number>): number {
  const upper = token.toUpperCase();
  if (nameMap && upper in nameMap) {
    return nameMap[upper];
  }
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(token)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid token: '${token}'`);
  }
  return parseInt(token, 10);
}

function validateField(
  fieldStr: string,
  bound: FieldBound
): void {
  if (!fieldStr || fieldStr.trim() === '') {
    throw new Error(`Field '${bound.name}' cannot be empty`);
  }

  const items = fieldStr.split(',');
  for (const item of items) {
    if (!item) {
      throw new Error(`Empty element in field '${bound.name}'`);
    }

    let base = item;
    if (item.includes('/')) {
      const parts = item.split('/');
      if (parts.length !== 2) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid step expression '${item}' in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      base = parts[0];
      const stepStr = parts[1];
      if (!/^\d+$/.test(stepStr)) {
        throw new Error(`Step '${stepStr}' must be a positive integer in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      const step = parseInt(stepStr, 10);
      if (step < 1) {
        throw new Error(`Step must be >= 1 in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      if (base === '*') {
        continue;
      }
    }

    if (base === '*') {
      continue;
    }

    if (base.includes('-')) {
      const rangeParts = base.split('-');
      if (rangeParts.length !== 2) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid range expression '${base}' in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      const start = parseToken(rangeParts[0], bound.nameMap);
      const end = parseToken(rangeParts[1], bound.nameMap);
      if (start < bound.min || start > bound.max || end < bound.min || end > bound.max) {
        throw new Error(`Range values '${base}' must be between ${bound.min} and ${bound.max} in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      if (start > end) {
        throw new Error(`Range start '${start}' must be <= end '${end}' in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
    } else {
      const val = parseToken(base, bound.nameMap);
      if (val < bound.min || val > bound.max) {
        throw new Error(`Value '${val}' out of range [${bound.min}, ${bound.max}] in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
    }
  }
}

export function cronValidator(): ValidatorFn {
  return (control: AbstractControl): ValidationErrors | null => {
    const value = control.value;
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
      return null;
    }

    if (typeof value !== 'string') {
      return { invalidCron: { message: 'Cron expression must be a string' } };
    }

    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (trimmed === '') {
      return null;
    }

    const fields = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
    if (fields.length !== 5) {
      return {
        invalidCron: {
          message: `Expected exactly 5 fields (<minute> <hour> <day of month> <month> <day of week>), got ${fields.length}`,
        },
      };
    }

    try {
      for (let i = 0; i < FIELD_BOUNDS.length; i++) {
        validateField(fields[i], FIELD_BOUNDS[i]);
      }
      return null;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Invalid cron syntax';
      return {
        invalidCron: {
          message: errorMsg,
        },
      };
    }
  };
}
