=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\schedules\schedule-management.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule, ReactiveFormsModule, FormBuilder, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { cronValidator } from '../connections/validators/cron.validator';
import { DatabaseConnection } from '../connections/models/connection.models';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../catalog/models/catalog.models';

export interface DatabaseNodeItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  engine_type: string;
  database_name: string;
  category_name?: string;
  host?: string;
  port?: number;
  owner?: string | null;
  is_active?: boolean;
}

export interface DbTreeCategoryNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  connections: DatabaseConnection[];
  children: DbTreeCategoryNode[];
  hasMatchingDescendants: boolean;
  totalDatabasesCount: number;
  isUncategorized?: boolean;
}

export interface SyncScheduleData {
  id: string;
  connection_id: string;
  cron_expression: string;
  is_enabled: boolean;
  last_run_at?: string | null;
  last_run_status?: string | null;
  last_run_message?: string | null;
  next_run_at?: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface CronPreset {
  label: string;
  expression: string;
  description: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-schedule-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, ReactiveFormsModule],
  templateUrl: './schedule-management.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./schedule-management.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class ScheduleManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  // States
  readonly isLoadingTree = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isLoadingSchedule = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isSavingSchedule = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isTriggeringSync = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Backend state for categories & connections
  readonly rawCategories = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  readonly connections = signal<DatabaseConnection[]>([]);

  // Multi-criteria tree filtering (matching DbUserMonitorComponent and CatalogExplorerComponent)
  readonly filterConnectionName = signal<string>('');
  readonly filterDatabaseName = signal<string>('');
  readonly filterHost = signal<string>('');
  readonly filterOwner = signal<string>('');
  readonly filterEngineType = signal<string>('');

  // Tree expansion state
  readonly expandedCategoryIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set<string>());

  // Check whether any tree filter is active
  readonly hasActiveTreeFilters = computed<boolean>(() => {
    return Boolean(
      this.filterConnectionName().trim() ||
      this.filterDatabaseName().trim() ||
      this.filterHost().trim() ||
      this.filterOwner().trim() ||
      this.filterEngineType()
    );
  });

  // Predicate to verify if a connection matches all active multi-criteria filters
  matchesConnection(conn: DatabaseConnection): boolean {
    const nameQ = this.filterConnectionName().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (nameQ && !conn.name.toLowerCase().includes(nameQ)) {
      return false;
    }

    const dbNameQ = this.filterDatabaseName().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (dbNameQ && !conn.database_name.toLowerCase().includes(dbNameQ)) {
      return false;
    }

    const hostQ = this.filterHost().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (hostQ && !conn.host.toLowerCase().includes(hostQ)) {
      return false;
    }

    const ownerQ = this.filterOwner().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (ownerQ && (!conn.owner || !conn.owner.toLowerCase().includes(ownerQ))) {
      return false;
    }

    const engine = this.filterEngineType();
    if (engine && conn.engine_type.toUpperCase() !== engine.toUpperCase()) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  }

  // Hierarchical Category -> Subcategories -> Databases Tree
  readonly displayTree = computed<DbTreeCategoryNode[]>(() => {
    const rawCategories = this.rawCategories();
    const allConns = this.connections();
    const activeFilters = this.hasActiveTreeFilters();
    const connMap = new Map<string, DatabaseConnection>(allConns.map((c) => [c.id, c]));

    const buildCategoryNode = (cat: CategoryTreeNode): DbTreeCategoryNode => {
      const assigned: DatabaseConnection[] = [];
      const seenIds = new Set<string>();

      if (cat.databases) {
        for (const dbInfo of cat.databases) {
          const c = connMap.get(dbInfo.id);
          if (c && !seenIds.has(c.id)) {
            seenIds.add(c.id);
            assigned.push(c);
          }
        }
      }

      for (const c of allConns) {
        if (c.category_id === cat.id && !seenIds.has(c.id)) {
          seenIds.add(c.id);
          assigned.push(c);
        }
      }

      const matchingConnections = activeFilters
        ? assigned.filter((c) => this.matchesConnection(c))
        : assigned;

      const matchingChildren: DbTreeCategoryNode[] = [];
      for (const child of cat.children || []) {
        const childNode = buildCategoryNode(child);
        if (!activeFilters || childNode.hasMatchingDescendants || childNode.connections.length > 0) {
          matchingChildren.push(childNode);
        }
      }

      const hasMatch =
        matchingConnections.length > 0 ||
        matchingChildren.some((child) => child.hasMatchingDescendants || child.connections.length > 0);

      const totalDbs =
        assigned.length +
        matchingChildren.reduce((acc, child) => acc + child.totalDatabasesCount, 0);

      return {
        id: cat.id,
        name: cat.name,
        description: cat.description,
        connections: matchingConnections,
        children: matchingChildren,
        hasMatchingDescendants: hasMatch,
        totalDatabasesCount: totalDbs,
      };
    };

    const tree: DbTreeCategoryNode[] = [];
    for (const cat of rawCategories) {
      const node = buildCategoryNode(cat);
      if (!activeFilters || node.hasMatchingDescendants || node.connections.length > 0) {
        tree.push(node);
      }
    }

    return tree;
  });

  // Automatic آ«ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡آ» branch for uncategorized databases
  readonly uncategorizedNode = computed<DbTreeCategoryNode | null>(() => {
    const rawCategories = this.rawCategories();
    const allConns = this.connections();
    const activeFilters = this.hasActiveTreeFilters();

    const assignedIds = new Set<string>();
    const collectAssignedIds = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[]) => {
      for (const cat of nodes) {
        if (cat.databases) {
          for (const db of cat.databases) {
            assignedIds.add(db.id);
          }
        }
        if (cat.children) {
          collectAssignedIds(cat.children);
        }
      }
    };
    collectAssignedIds(rawCategories);

    for (const c of allConns) {
      if (c.category_id) {
        assignedIds.add(c.id);
      }
    }

    const uncatConns = allConns.filter((c) => !assignedIds.has(c.id));
    if (uncatConns.length === 0) return null;

    const matchingUncat = activeFilters
      ? uncatConns.filter((c) => this.matchesConnection(c))
      : uncatConns;

    if (activeFilters && matchingUncat.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return {
      id: 'uncategorized',
      name: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡',
      description: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ ظ‡غŒع† ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹غŒ ظ…ظ†طھط³ط¨ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯',
      connections: matchingUncat,
      children: [],
      hasMatchingDescendants: matchingUncat.length > 0,
      totalDatabasesCount: uncatConns.length,
      isUncategorized: true,
    };
  });

  // Total matching databases count across visible tree
  readonly totalFilteredDatabasesCount = computed<number>(() => {
    const tree = this.displayTree();
    const uncat = this.uncategorizedNode();
    let sum = 0;
    const countConns = (node: DbTreeCategoryNode) => {
      sum += node.connections.length;
      for (const ch of node.children) {
        countConns(ch);
      }
    };
    for (const root of tree) {
      countConns(root);
    }
    if (uncat) {
      sum += uncat.connections.length;
    }
    return sum;
  });

  // Selected Database & Schedule
  readonly selectedDatabase = signal<DatabaseNodeItem | null>(null);
  readonly currentSchedule = signal<SyncScheduleData | null>(null);

  // Feedback Toasts
  readonly toastMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly toastType = signal<'success' | 'error'>('success');
  private toastTimer: any = null;

  // Cron Presets
  readonly cronPresets: CronPreset[] = [
    { label: 'ظ‡ط± غ¶ ط³ط§ط¹طھ', expression: '0 */6 * * *', description: 'ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± غ¶ ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط¯ط± ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ طµظپط±' },
    { label: 'ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡ (غ² ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯)', expression: '0 2 * * *', description: 'ظ‡ط± ط´ط¨ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ²:غ°غ° ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯' },
    { label: 'ظ‡ظپطھع¯غŒ (ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§)', expression: '0 1 * * 5', description: 'ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ±:غ°غ° ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯' },
    { label: 'ظ…ط§ظ‡ط§ظ†ظ‡ (ط±ظˆط² ط§ظˆظ„)', expression: '0 0 1 * *', description: 'ط§ط¨طھط¯ط§غŒ ظ‡ط± ظ…ط§ظ‡ طھظ‚ظˆغŒظ…غŒ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ°غ°:غ°غ°' },
    { label: 'ظ‡ط± غ±غµ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡', expression: '*/15 * * * *', description: 'ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± غ±غµ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± (ظ¾ط§غŒط´ ظ…ط¯ط§ظˆظ…)' },
  ];

  // Form
  readonly scheduleForm = this.fb.group({
    cron_expression: ['', [Validators.required, cronValidator()]],
    is_enabled: [true],
  });

  readonly currentCronExpression = signal<string>('');

  constructor() {
    this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.valueChanges.subscribe((val) => {
      this.currentCronExpression.set(val || '');
    });
  }

  // Human readable live Persian preview of cron
  readonly cronHumanPreview = computed<string>(() => {
    const expr = (this.currentCronExpression().trim() || this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.value?.trim()) || '';
    if (!expr) return 'ط¹ط¨ط§ط±طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.';

    const matched = this.cronPresets.find((p) => p.expression === expr);
    if (matched) return matched.description;

    const parts = expr.split(/\s+/);
    if (parts.length !== 5) return 'ظپط±ظ…طھ ع©ط±ظˆظ† ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ط§ظ‹ ط§ط² غµ ط¨ط®ط´ ظ…ط¬ط²ط§ ط¨ط§ ظپط§طµظ„ظ‡ طھط´ع©غŒظ„ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.';

    const [min, hour, dom, month, dow] = parts;
    if (min === '0' && hour.startsWith('*/')) {
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± ${hour.replace('*/', '')} ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط¯ط± ط§ط¨طھط¯ط§غŒ ط³ط§ط¹طھ`;
    }
    if (min.startsWith('*/') && hour === '*' && dom === '*' && month === '*' && dow === '*') {
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± ${min.replace('*/', '')} ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± ط¯ط± طھظ…ط§ظ… ط³ط§ط¹ط§طھ`;
    }
    if (!isNaN(Number(min)) && !isNaN(Number(hour)) && dom === '*' && month === '*' && dow === '*') {
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± ط±ظˆط² ط±ط§ط³ ط³ط§ط¹طھ ${hour.padStart(2, '0')}:${min.padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
    if (!isNaN(Number(min)) && !isNaN(Number(hour)) && dom === '*' && month === '*' && !isNaN(Number(dow))) {
      const days = ['غŒع©â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ع†ظ‡ط§ط±ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ظ¾ظ†ط¬â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡', 'ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡'];
      const dayName = days[Number(dow)] || `ط±ظˆط² ${dow}`;
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ط¯ط± ${dayName}â€Œظ‡ط§ ط±ط§ط³ ط³ط§ط¹طھ ${hour.padStart(2, '0')}:${min.padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
    return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ط¨ط± ط§ط³ط§ط³ ط§ظ„ع¯ظˆغŒ ط§ط®طھطµط§طµغŒ: ${expr}`;
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadCategoryTree();
  }

  loadCategoryTree(): void {
    this.isLoadingTree.set(true);
    forkJoin({
      categories: this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree'),
      connections: this.http.get<DatabaseConnection[]>('/api/v1/connections'),
    }).subscribe({
      next: ({ categories, connections }) => {
        this.rawCategories.set(categories || []);
        this.connections.set(connections || []);
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);

        // Auto-expand all top-level categories and uncategorized branch initially
        const initialExpanded = new Set<string>();
        (categories || []).forEach((c) => initialExpanded.add(c.id));
        initialExpanded.add('uncategorized');
        this.expandedCategoryIds.set(initialExpanded);

        // Auto-select first connection if any
        if (connections && connections.length > 0) {
          const firstConn = connections[0];
          const cat = (categories || []).find((c) => c.id === firstConn.category_id);
          this.selectConnection(firstConn, cat?.name);
        }
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);
        this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¯ط±غŒط§ظپطھ ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  isCategoryExpanded(node: DbTreeCategoryNode): boolean {
    if (this.hasActiveTreeFilters()) {
      return node.hasMatchingDescendants || node.connections.length > 0;
    }
    return this.expandedCategoryIds().has(node.id);
  }

  toggleCategory(node: DbTreeCategoryNode, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    const currentlyExpanded = this.isCategoryExpanded(node);
    const next = new Set(this.expandedCategoryIds());
    if (currentlyExpanded) {
      next.delete(node.id);
    } else {
      next.add(node.id);
    }
    this.expandedCategoryIds.set(next);
  }

  clearTreeFilters(): void {
    this.filterConnectionName.set('');
    this.filterDatabaseName.set('');
    this.filterHost.set('');
    this.filterOwner.set('');
    this.filterEngineType.set('');
  }

  selectConnection(conn: DatabaseConnection, categoryName?: string): void {
    const dbItem: DatabaseNodeItem = {
      id: conn.id,
      name: conn.name,
      engine_type: conn.engine_type,
      database_name: conn.database_name,
      category_name: categoryName || 'ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ط¯ط³طھظ‡',
      host: conn.host,
      port: conn.port,
      owner: conn.owner,
      is_active: conn.is_active,
    };
    this.selectDatabase(dbItem);
  }

  selectDatabase(db: DatabaseNodeItem): void {
    this.selectedDatabase.set(db);
    this.isLoadingSchedule.set(true);
    this.http.get<SyncScheduleData>(`/api/v1/connections/${db.id}/schedule`).subscribe({
      next: (schedule) => {
        this.currentSchedule.set(schedule);
        const cron = schedule.cron_expression || '0 2 * * *';
        this.scheduleForm.patchValue({
          cron_expression: cron,
          is_enabled: schedule.is_enabled ?? true,
        });
        this.currentCronExpression.set(cron);
        this.isLoadingSchedule.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingSchedule.set(false);
        this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط¬ط²ط¦غŒط§طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  applyPreset(preset: CronPreset): void {
    this.scheduleForm.patchValue({
      cron_expression: preset.expression,
    });
    this.currentCronExpression.set(preset.expression);
    this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.markAsDirty();
  }

  saveSchedule(): void {
    const db = this.selectedDatabase();
    if (!db || this.scheduleForm.invalid) {
      this.scheduleForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isSavingSchedule.set(true);
    const payload = {
      cron_expression: this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.value?.trim(),
      is_enabled: this.scheduleForm.controls.is_enabled.value ?? true,
    };

    this.http.put<SyncScheduleData>(`/api/v1/connections/${db.id}/schedule`, payload).subscribe({
      next: (updated) => {
        this.currentSchedule.set(updated);
        this.isSavingSchedule.set(false);
        this.showToast('طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯.', 'success');
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isSavingSchedule.set(false);
        const msg = err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ';
        this.showToast(msg, 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  triggerSyncNow(): void {
    const db = this.selectedDatabase();
    if (!db) return;

    this.isTriggeringSync.set(true);
    this.http.post<SyncScheduleData>(`/api/v1/connections/${db.id}/sync`, {}).subscribe({
      next: (updated) => {
        this.currentSchedule.set(updated);
        this.isTriggeringSync.set(false);
        this.showToast(`ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ظ…طھط§ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ آ«${db.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯.`, 'success');
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isTriggeringSync.set(false);
        const msg = err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒ ظپط±ط¢غŒظ†ط¯ ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ';
        this.showToast(msg, 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  showToast(message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' = 'success'): void {
    if (this.toastTimer) clearTimeout(this.toastTimer);
    this.toastMessage.set(message);
    this.toastType.set(type);
    this.toastTimer = setTimeout(() => this.toastMessage.set(null), 4500);
  }

  closeToast(): void {
    this.toastMessage.set(null);
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnChanges,
  SimpleChanges,
  inject,
  input,
  model,
  signal,
} from "@angular/core";
import { CommonModule } from "@angular/common";
import {
  SyncJobDetailResponse,
  SyncJobService,
} from "../../core/services/sync-job.service";

@Component({
  selector: "app-sync-job-diagnostic-modal",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  templateUrl: "./sync-job-diagnostic-modal.component.html",
  styleUrls: ["./sync-job-diagnostic-modal.component.css"],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class SyncJobDiagnosticModalComponent implements OnChanges {
  private readonly syncJobService = inject(SyncJobService);

  readonly jobId = input<string | null>(null);
  readonly visible = model<boolean>(false);

  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly jobDetail = signal<SyncJobDetailResponse | null>(null);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly copied = signal<boolean>(false);

  ngOnChanges(changes: SimpleChanges): void {
    const jobId = this.jobId();
    if (changes["visible"] && this.visible() && jobId) {
      this.loadDetail(jobId);
    }
  }

  loadDetail(id: string): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);
    this.copied.set(false);

    this.syncJobService.getSyncJobDetail(id).subscribe({
      next: (detail) => {
        this.jobDetail.set(detail);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg =
          err instanceof Error
            ? err.message
            : "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط¬ط²ط¦غŒط§طھ طھط´ط®غŒطµغŒ ط¬ط§ط¨ ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ";
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  copyErrorTrace(): void {
    const detail = this.jobDetail();
    if (!detail) return;

    const payload = JSON.stringify(detail.error_trace_jsonb || {}, null, 2);
    if (navigator?.clipboard?.writeText) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(payload).then(() => {
        this.copied.set(true);
        setTimeout(() => this.copied.set(false), 3000);
      });
    }
  }

  close(): void {
    this.visible.set(false);
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\sync-monitor\sync-monitor.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnDestroy,
  OnInit,
  inject,
  signal,
} from "@angular/core";

import { FormsModule } from "@angular/forms";
import { Subject, Subscription, timer } from "rxjs";
import { switchMap, takeUntil } from "rxjs/operators";
import {
  SyncJobListResponse,
  SyncJobResponse,
  SyncJobService,
  SyncJobStatus,
} from "../../core/services/sync-job.service";
import { SyncJobDiagnosticModalComponent } from "./sync-job-diagnostic-modal.component";

@Component({
  selector: "app-sync-monitor",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, SyncJobDiagnosticModalComponent],
  templateUrl: "./sync-monitor.component.html",
  styleUrls: ["./sync-monitor.component.css"],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class SyncMonitorComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  private readonly syncJobService = inject(SyncJobService);
  private readonly destroy$ = new Subject<void>();
  private pollingSub: Subscription | null = null;

  readonly jobs = signal<SyncJobResponse[]>([]);
  readonly totalJobs = signal<number>(0);
  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isPollingActive = signal<boolean>(true);
  readonly selectedStatus = signal<string>("ALL");
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly successMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly retryingJobId = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Diagnostic Modal
  readonly selectedJobIdForDiag = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly showDiagModal = signal<boolean>(false);

  private readonly persianDateFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    second: "2-digit",
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.startPolling();
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.stopPolling();
    this.destroy$.next();
    this.destroy$.complete();
  }

  startPolling(): void {
    this.stopPolling();
    this.isPollingActive.set(true);
    this.fetchJobsOnce();

    this.pollingSub = timer(3000, 3000)
      .pipe(
        takeUntil(this.destroy$),
        switchMap(() => {
          const status = this.selectedStatus();
          return this.syncJobService.getSyncJobs(
            status === "ALL" ? undefined : status,
            undefined,
            undefined,
            50,
          );
        }),
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: (res: SyncJobListResponse) => {
          this.jobs.set(res.items);
          this.totalJobs.set(res.total);
          this.isLoading.set(false);
        },
        error: (err: unknown) => {
          const msg =
            err instanceof Error
              ? err.message
              : "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظˆط§ع©ط´غŒ ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط¬ط§ط¨â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ";
          this.errorMessage.set(msg);
          this.isLoading.set(false);
        },
      });
  }

  stopPolling(): void {
    if (this.pollingSub) {
      this.pollingSub.unsubscribe();
      this.pollingSub = null;
    }
    this.isPollingActive.set(false);
  }

  togglePolling(): void {
    if (this.isPollingActive()) {
      this.stopPolling();
    } else {
      this.startPolling();
    }
  }

  onStatusFilterChange(status: string): void {
    this.selectedStatus.set(status);
    this.fetchJobsOnce();
  }

  fetchJobsOnce(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    const status = this.selectedStatus();
    this.syncJobService
      .getSyncJobs(
        status === "ALL" ? undefined : status,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        50,
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: (res: SyncJobListResponse) => {
          this.jobs.set(res.items);
          this.totalJobs.set(res.total);
          this.isLoading.set(false);
        },
        error: (err: unknown) => {
          const msg =
            err instanceof Error ? err.message : "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ظ‡â€Œط±ظˆط²ط±ط³ط§ظ†غŒ ط¬ط§ط¨â€Œظ‡ط§";
          this.errorMessage.set(msg);
          this.isLoading.set(false);
        },
      });
  }

  onRetryJob(job: SyncJobResponse): void {
    this.retryingJobId.set(job.id);
    this.successMessage.set(null);

    this.syncJobService.retrySyncJob(job.id).subscribe({
      next: (newJob) => {
        this.retryingJobId.set(null);
        this.successMessage.set(
          `طھظ„ط§ط´ ظ…ط¬ط¯ط¯ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط¬ط§ط¨ ${job.id.substring(0, 8)} ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ (ط´ظ†ط§ط³ظ‡ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯: ${newJob.id.substring(0, 8)})`,
        );
        this.fetchJobsOnce();
        setTimeout(() => this.successMessage.set(null), 5000);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        this.retryingJobId.set(null);
        const msg =
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : "ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط«ط¨طھ طھظ„ط§ط´ ظ…ط¬ط¯ط¯ ط¬ط§ط¨";
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        setTimeout(() => this.errorMessage.set(null), 5000);
      },
    });
  }

  openDiagnostics(jobId: string): void {
    this.selectedJobIdForDiag.set(jobId);
    this.showDiagModal.set(true);
  }

  formatPersianDate(isoDate?: string | null): string {
    if (!isoDate) return "â€”";
    try {
      return this.persianDateFormatter.format(new Date(isoDate));
    } catch {
      return isoDate;
    }
  }

  getStatusBadgeClass(status: SyncJobStatus): string {
    switch (status) {
      case "SUCCESS":
        return "badge-status-success";
      case "PARTIAL_FAILURE":
        return "badge-status-partial_failure";
      case "FAILED":
        return "badge-status-failed";
      case "RUNNING":
        return "badge-status-running";
      case "QUEUED":
      default:
        return "badge-status-queued";
    }
  }

  getStatusLabel(status: SyncJobStatus): string {
    switch (status) {
      case "SUCCESS":
        return "ظ…ظˆظپظ‚ (SUCCESS)";
      case "PARTIAL_FAILURE":
        return "ط´ع©ط³طھ ط¬ط²ط¦غŒ (PARTIAL_FAILURE)";
      case "FAILED":

      case "RUNNING":

      case "QUEUED":
        return "ط¯ط± طµظپ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± (QUEUED)";
      default:
        return status;
    }
  }

  getTriggerLabel(trigger: string): string {
    switch (trigger) {
      case "SCHEDULE":
        return "âڈ±ï¸ڈ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط®ظˆط¯ع©ط§ط±";
      case "MANUAL":

      case "RETRY":
        return "ًں”„ طھع©ط±ط§ط± ظ…ط¬ط¯ط¯ (Retry)";
      default:
        return trigger;
    }
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\taxonomy\services\category.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../../catalog/models/catalog.models';

export interface CategoryResponse {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
  assigned_tables_count: number;
}

export interface CategoryCreateRequest {
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status?: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
}

export interface CategoryUpdateRequest {
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status?: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
}

export interface CategoryAssignmentItem {
  id: string;
  category_id: string;
  category_name?: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  table_name?: string;
  schema_name?: string;
  database_name?: string;
  created_at?: string;
}

export interface AvailableTableItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  schema_name: string;
  database_name: string;
  row_count_estimate?: number;
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class CategoryService {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/categories';

  getCategoryTree(): Observable<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/tree`);
  }

  listCategories(): Observable<CategoryResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<CategoryResponse[]>(this.baseUrl);
  }

  createCategory(payload: CategoryCreateRequest): Observable<CategoryResponse> {
    return this.http.post<CategoryResponse>(this.baseUrl, payload);
  }

  updateCategory(id: string, payload: CategoryUpdateRequest): Observable<CategoryResponse> {
    return this.http.put<CategoryResponse>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, payload);
  }

  deleteCategory(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getAssignments(categoryId?: string, entityType = 'TABLE'): Observable<CategoryAssignmentItem[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (categoryId) {
      params = params.set('category_id', categoryId);
    }
    if (entityType) {
      params = params.set('entity_type', entityType);
    }
    return this.http.get<CategoryAssignmentItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/assignments`, { params });
  }

  assignTable(categoryId: string, tableId: string): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post(`${this.baseUrl}/assignments`, {
      category_id: categoryId,
      entity_type: 'TABLE',
      entity_id: tableId,
    });
  }

  unassignTable(categoryId: string, tableId: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/assignments/${categoryId}/TABLE/${tableId}`);
  }

  getAvailableTables(search?: string): Observable<AvailableTableItem[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (search && search.trim()) {
      params = params.set('search', search.trim());
    }
    return this.http.get<AvailableTableItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/available-tables`, { params });
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\taxonomy\taxonomy-management.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import {
  FormControl,
  FormGroup,
  FormsModule,
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  Validators,
} from '@angular/forms';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { TreeTableModule } from 'primeng/treetable';
import { TreeNode } from 'primeng/api';

import { TagItem, CategoryTreeNode } from '../catalog/models/catalog.models';
import {
  CategoryService,
  AvailableTableItem,
  CategoryAssignmentItem,
} from './services/category.service';

export interface CategoryRowData {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
  children_count: number;
  assigned_databases_count: number;
  assigned_tables_count: number;
  tables?: any[];
  databases?: any[];
  is_leaf?: boolean;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-taxonomy-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    FormsModule,
    ReactiveFormsModule,
    TreeTableModule,
  ],
  templateUrl: './taxonomy-management.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./taxonomy-management.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class TaxonomyManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private categoryService = inject(CategoryService);

  // Active Tab
  activeTab = signal<'categories' | 'tags'>('categories');

  // Loading States
  isLoadingTree = signal<boolean>(false);
  isLoadingTags = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSavingCategory = signal<boolean>(false);
  isLoadingAvailableTables = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Raw Categories & TreeNodes
  rawCategoryTree = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  expandedNodeKeys = signal<Set<string>>(new Set<string>());

  // Filter and Search
  searchTerm = signal<string>('');
  statusFilter = signal<'ALL' | 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE'>('ALL');

  // Mapped PrimeNG TreeNodes
  treeNodes = computed<TreeNode<CategoryRowData>[]>(() => {
    const raw = this.rawCategoryTree();
    const expanded = this.expandedNodeKeys();
    return this.buildTreeNodes(raw, expanded);
  });

  // Filtered TreeNodes
  filteredTreeNodes = computed<TreeNode<CategoryRowData>[]>(() => {
    const nodes = this.treeNodes();
    const term = this.searchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const status = this.statusFilter();

    if (!term && status === 'ALL') {
      return nodes;
    }

    return this.filterTreeRecursively(nodes, term, status);
  });

  // Category Statistics
  totalCategoriesCount = computed<number>(() => {
    return this.countAllCategories(this.rawCategoryTree());
  });

  totalAssignedTablesCount = computed<number>(() => {
    return this.countAllAssignedTables(this.rawCategoryTree());
  });

  // Category Modal State
  isCategoryModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  categoryModalMode = signal<'create-root' | 'create-child' | 'edit'>('create-root');
  selectedParentCategory = signal<CategoryRowData | null>(null);
  editingCategory = signal<CategoryRowData | null>(null);

  // Category Reactive Form
  categoryForm = new FormGroup({
    name: new FormControl<string>('', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1), Validators.maxLength(200)],
    }),
    description: new FormControl<string>(''),
    status: new FormControl<'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE'>('ACTIVE', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required],
    }),
  });

  // Delete Category Confirmation Modal State
  isDeleteModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  categoryToDelete = signal<CategoryRowData | null>(null);
  deleteSubcategoryCount = signal<number>(0);
  deleteTableCount = signal<number>(0);

  // Assign Tables Modal State
  isAssignModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  targetCategoryForAssign = signal<CategoryRowData | null>(null);
  currentAssignedTables = signal<CategoryAssignmentItem[]>([]);
  availableCatalogTables = signal<AvailableTableItem[]>([]);
  tableSearchQuery = signal<string>('');

  // Governed Tags State
  tags = signal<TagItem[]>([]);
  isTagModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  editingTagId = signal<string | null>(null);
  tagFormName = '';
  tagFormColor = '#3b82f6';
  tagFormDescription = '';
  colorPresets = [
    '#ef4444',
    '#f97316',
    '#f59e0b',
    '#10b981',
    '#06b6d4',
    '#3b82f6',
    '#6366f1',
    '#8b5cf6',
    '#ec4899',
    '#64748b',
  ];

  // Toast Notifications
  toastMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  toastType = signal<'success' | 'error'>('success');
  private toastTimer: any = null;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadCategoryTree();
  }

  // ==========================================
  // CATEGORY TREE LOADING & MAPPING
  // ==========================================

  loadCategoryTree(expandTargetId?: string): void {
    this.isLoadingTree.set(true);
    this.categoryService.getCategoryTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.rawCategoryTree.set(tree);
        if (expandTargetId) {
          this.expandNodeAndAncestors(tree, expandTargetId);
        }
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط¯ط±ط®طھ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  private buildTreeNodes(
    nodes: CategoryTreeNode[],
    expandedKeys: Set<string>
  ): TreeNode<CategoryRowData>[] {
    return nodes.map((node) => {
      const isExpanded = expandedKeys.has(node.id);
      const children = node.children && node.children.length > 0
        ? this.buildTreeNodes(node.children, expandedKeys)
        : [];

      const rowData: CategoryRowData = {
        id: node.id,
        name: node.name,
        description: node.description,
        parent_id: node.parent_id,
        status: (node.status as 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE') || 'ACTIVE',
        children_count: node.children_count !== undefined ? node.children_count : children.length,
        assigned_databases_count:
          node.assigned_databases_count !== undefined
            ? node.assigned_databases_count
            : node.databases
              ? node.databases.length
              : 0,
        assigned_tables_count:
          node.assigned_tables_count !== undefined
            ? node.assigned_tables_count
            : node.tables
              ? node.tables.length
              : 0,
        tables: node.tables || [],
        databases: node.databases || [],
        is_leaf: children.length === 0,
      };

      return {
        key: node.id,
        label: node.name,
        data: rowData,
        expanded: isExpanded,
        children: children,
        leaf: children.length === 0,
      };
    });
  }

  private filterTreeRecursively(
    nodes: TreeNode<CategoryRowData>[],
    term: string,
    status: 'ALL' | 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE'
  ): TreeNode<CategoryRowData>[] {
    const result: TreeNode<CategoryRowData>[] = [];

    for (const node of nodes) {
      const data = node.data!;
      const matchesTerm = !term || data.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) || (data.description && data.description.toLowerCase().includes(term));
      const matchesStatus = status === 'ALL' || data.status === status;

      const filteredChildren = node.children && node.children.length > 0
        ? this.filterTreeRecursively(node.children, term, status)
        : [];

      if ((matchesTerm && matchesStatus) || filteredChildren.length > 0) {
        result.push({
          ...node,
          expanded: term ? true : node.expanded,
          children: filteredChildren,
        });
      }
    }

    return result;
  }

  private expandNodeAndAncestors(tree: CategoryTreeNode[], targetId: string): void {
    const path: string[] = [];

    const findPath = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[], currentPath: string[]): boolean => {
      for (const n of nodes) {
        const nextPath = [...currentPath, n.id];
        if (n.id === targetId) {
          path.push(...nextPath);
          return true;
        }
        if (n.children && n.children.length > 0) {
          if (findPath(n.children, nextPath)) {
            return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    };

    findPath(tree, []);
    if (path.length > 0) {
      this.expandedNodeKeys.update((keys) => {
        const updated = new Set(keys);
        path.forEach((id) => updated.add(id));
        return updated;
      });
    }
  }

  private countAllCategories(nodes: CategoryTreeNode[]): number {
    let count = 0;
    for (const n of nodes) {
      count += 1;
      if (n.children && n.children.length > 0) {
        count += this.countAllCategories(n.children);
      }
    }
    return count;
  }

  private countAllAssignedTables(nodes: CategoryTreeNode[]): number {
    let count = 0;
    for (const n of nodes) {
      count += n.assigned_tables_count || (n.tables ? n.tables.length : 0);
      if (n.children && n.children.length > 0) {
        count += this.countAllAssignedTables(n.children);
      }
    }
    return count;
  }

  // Node toggle event from TreeTable
  onNodeToggle(event: any): void {
    const node = event?.node || event;
    if (!node || !node.key) return;
    this.expandedNodeKeys.update((keys) => {
      const updated = new Set(keys);
      if (node.expanded) {
        updated.add(node.key!);
      } else {
        updated.delete(node.key!);
      }
      return updated;
    });
  }

  expandAll(): void {
    const allIds = new Set<string>();
    const collectIds = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[]) => {
      for (const n of nodes) {
        allIds.add(n.id);
        if (n.children && n.children.length > 0) {
          collectIds(n.children);
        }
      }
    };
    collectIds(this.rawCategoryTree());
    this.expandedNodeKeys.set(allIds);
  }

  collapseAll(): void {
    this.expandedNodeKeys.set(new Set<string>());
  }

  // ==========================================
  // CREATE / EDIT CATEGORY MODAL
  // ==========================================

  openAddRootModal(): void {
    this.categoryModalMode.set('create-root');
    this.selectedParentCategory.set(null);
    this.editingCategory.set(null);
    this.categoryForm.reset({
      name: '',
      description: '',
      status: 'ACTIVE',
    });
    this.isCategoryModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  openAddSubcategoryModal(parent: CategoryRowData): void {
    this.categoryModalMode.set('create-child');
    this.selectedParentCategory.set(parent);
    this.editingCategory.set(null);
    this.categoryForm.reset({
      name: '',
      description: '',
      status: 'ACTIVE',
    });
    this.isCategoryModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  openEditCategoryModal(category: CategoryRowData): void {
    this.categoryModalMode.set('edit');
    this.selectedParentCategory.set(null);
    this.editingCategory.set(category);
    this.categoryForm.setValue({
      name: category.name,
      description: category.description || '',
      status: category.status,
    });
    this.isCategoryModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeCategoryModal(): void {
    this.isCategoryModalOpen.set(false);
    this.selectedParentCategory.set(null);
    this.editingCategory.set(null);
    this.categoryForm.reset();
  }

  submitCategoryForm(): void {
    if (this.categoryForm.invalid) {
      this.categoryForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    const { name, description, status } = this.categoryForm.getRawValue();
    this.isSavingCategory.set(true);

    const mode = this.categoryModalMode();

    if (mode === 'create-root') {
      this.categoryService
        .createCategory({
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description?.trim() || null,
          parent_id: null,
          status: status,
        })
        .subscribe({
          next: (created) => {
            this.isSavingCategory.set(false);
            this.closeCategoryModal();
            this.showToast(`ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط§طµظ„غŒ آ«${created.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ط´ط¯`, 'success');
            this.loadCategoryTree(created.id);
          },
          error: (err) => {
            this.isSavingCategory.set(false);
            this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط§طµظ„غŒ'), 'error');
          },
        });
    } else if (mode === 'create-child') {
      const parent = this.selectedParentCategory();
      if (!parent) return;

      this.categoryService
        .createCategory({
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description?.trim() || null,
          parent_id: parent.id,
          status: status,
        })
        .subscribe({
          next: (created) => {
            this.isSavingCategory.set(false);
            this.closeCategoryModal();
            this.showToast(
              `ط²غŒط±ط¯ط³طھظ‡ آ«${created.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط¨ظ‡ آ«${parent.name}آ» ط§ظپط²ظˆط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯`,
              'success'
            );
            // Automatically expand parent node
            this.expandedNodeKeys.update((keys) => {
              const updated = new Set(keys);
              updated.add(parent.id);
              return updated;
            });
            this.loadCategoryTree(created.id);
          },
          error: (err) => {
            this.isSavingCategory.set(false);
            this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ط²غŒط±ط¯ط³طھظ‡'), 'error');
          },
        });
    } else if (mode === 'edit') {
      const category = this.editingCategory();
      if (!category) return;

      this.categoryService
        .updateCategory(category.id, {
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description?.trim() || null,
          parent_id: category.parent_id || null,
          status: status,
        })
        .subscribe({
          next: (updated) => {
            this.isSavingCategory.set(false);
            this.closeCategoryModal();
            this.showToast(`طھط؛غŒغŒط±ط§طھ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ آ«${updated.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯`, 'success');
            this.loadCategoryTree(updated.id);
          },
          error: (err) => {
            this.isSavingCategory.set(false);
            this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ'), 'error');
          },
        });
    }
  }

  // ==========================================
  // SAFE DELETION CONFIRMATION
  // ==========================================

  confirmDeleteCategory(category: CategoryRowData): void {
    this.categoryToDelete.set(category);

    // Calculate subcategory count
    const findNode = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[], id: string): CategoryTreeNode | null => {
      for (const n of nodes) {
        if (n.id === id) return n;
        if (n.children) {
          const found = findNode(n.children, id);
          if (found) return found;
        }
      }
      return null;
    };

    const targetNode = findNode(this.rawCategoryTree(), category.id);
    const subCount = targetNode?.children?.length || 0;
    const tableCount = category.assigned_tables_count || 0;

    this.deleteSubcategoryCount.set(subCount);
    this.deleteTableCount.set(tableCount);
    this.isDeleteModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeDeleteModal(): void {
    this.isDeleteModalOpen.set(false);
    this.categoryToDelete.set(null);
  }

  executeDeleteCategory(): void {
    const cat = this.categoryToDelete();
    if (!cat) return;

    if (this.deleteSubcategoryCount() > 0) {
      this.showToast('ط§ظ…ع©ط§ظ† ط­ط°ظپ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط¯ط§ط±ط§غŒ ط²غŒط±ظ…ط¬ظ…ظˆط¹ظ‡ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯', 'error');
      return;
    }

    this.categoryService.deleteCategory(cat.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.closeDeleteModal();
        this.showToast(`ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ آ«${cat.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط­ط°ظپ ط´ط¯`, 'success');
        this.loadCategoryTree();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.closeDeleteModal();
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط§ظ…ع©ط§ظ† ط­ط°ظپ ط§غŒظ† ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  // ==========================================
  // ASSIGN TABLES MODAL (`ط§ط®طھطµط§طµ ط¬ط¯ط§ظˆظ„`)
  // ==========================================

  openAssignTablesModal(category: CategoryRowData): void {
    this.targetCategoryForAssign.set(category);
    this.tableSearchQuery.set('');
    this.isAssignModalOpen.set(true);
    this.loadCategoryAssignments(category.id);
    this.searchAvailableTables('');
  }

  closeAssignModal(): void {
    this.isAssignModalOpen.set(false);
    this.targetCategoryForAssign.set(null);
    this.currentAssignedTables.set([]);
    this.availableCatalogTables.set([]);
  }

  loadCategoryAssignments(categoryId: string): void {
    this.categoryService.getAssignments(categoryId, 'TABLE').subscribe({
      next: (assignments) => {
        this.currentAssignedTables.set(assignments);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط¬ط¯ط§ظˆظ„ ط§ط®طھطµط§طµâ€ŒغŒط§ظپطھظ‡'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  searchAvailableTables(query: string): void {
    this.isLoadingAvailableTables.set(true);
    this.categoryService.getAvailableTables(query).subscribe({
      next: (tables) => {
        this.availableCatalogTables.set(tables);
        this.isLoadingAvailableTables.set(false);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoadingAvailableTables.set(false);
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¬ط³طھط¬ظˆغŒ ط¬ط¯ط§ظˆظ„ ع©ط§طھط§ظ„ظˆع¯'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  onTableSearchInput(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const query = input.value;
    this.tableSearchQuery.set(query);
    this.searchAvailableTables(query);
  }

  isTableAssigned(tableId: string): boolean {
    return this.currentAssignedTables().some((a) => a.entity_id === tableId);
  }

  assignTableToCurrentCategory(table: AvailableTableItem): void {
    const cat = this.targetCategoryForAssign();
    if (!cat) return;

    if (this.isTableAssigned(table.id)) {
      this.showToast(`ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ آ«${table.name}آ» ظ¾غŒط´ ط§ط² ط§غŒظ† ط§ط®طھطµط§طµ غŒط§ظپطھظ‡ ط§ط³طھ`, 'error');
      return;
    }

    this.categoryService.assignTable(cat.id, table.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.showToast(`ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ آ«${table.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط¨ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط§ط®طھطµط§طµ غŒط§ظپطھ`, 'success');
        this.loadCategoryAssignments(cat.id);
        this.loadCategoryTree(cat.id);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§ط®طھطµط§طµ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ ط¨ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  unassignTableFromCurrentCategory(assignment: CategoryAssignmentItem): void {
    const cat = this.targetCategoryForAssign();
    if (!cat) return;

    this.categoryService.unassignTable(cat.id, assignment.entity_id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.showToast(`طھط®طµغŒطµ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ آ«${assignment.table_name || 'ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ط´ط¯ظ‡'}آ» ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯`, 'success');
        this.loadCategoryAssignments(cat.id);
        this.loadCategoryTree(cat.id);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ„ط؛ظˆ طھط®طµغŒطµ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  // ==========================================
  // GOVERNED TAGS METHODS (Preserved)
  // ==========================================

  loadTags(): void {
    this.isLoadingTags.set(true);
    this.http.get<TagItem[]>('/api/v1/tags').subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.tags.set(data);
        this.isLoadingTags.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingTags.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  openCreateTagModal(): void {
    this.editingTagId.set(null);
    this.tagFormName = '';
    this.tagFormColor = '#3b82f6';
    this.tagFormDescription = '';
    this.isTagModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  openEditTagModal(tag: TagItem): void {
    this.editingTagId.set(tag.id);
    this.tagFormName = tag.name;
    this.tagFormColor = tag.color;
    this.tagFormDescription = tag.description || '';
    this.isTagModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeTagModal(): void {
    this.isTagModalOpen.set(false);
  }

  saveTag(): void {
    if (!this.tagFormName.trim()) return;

    const payload = {
      name: this.tagFormName.trim(),
      color: this.tagFormColor,
      description: this.tagFormDescription.trim() || null,
      is_governed: true,
    };

    const id = this.editingTagId();
    if (id) {
      this.http.put(`/api/v1/tags/${id}`, payload).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.closeTagModal();
          this.loadTags();
          this.showToast('ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط´ط¯', 'success');
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨'), 'error');
        },
      });
    } else {
      this.http.post('/api/v1/tags', payload).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.closeTagModal();
          this.loadTags();
          this.showToast('ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ط´ط¯', 'success');
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨'), 'error');
        },
      });
    }
  }

  deleteTag(tag: TagItem): void {
    if (!confirm(`ط¢غŒط§ ط§ط² ط­ط°ظپ ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨ آ«${tag.name}آ» ط§ط·ظ…غŒظ†ط§ظ† ط¯ط§ط±غŒط¯طں`)) return;
    this.http.delete(`/api/v1/tags/${tag.id}`).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.loadTags();
        this.showToast('ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨ ط­ط°ظپ ط´ط¯', 'success');
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.showToast(this.extractErrorMessage(err, 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ ط¨ط±ع†ط³ط¨'), 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  // ==========================================
  // HELPERS
  // ==========================================

  showToast(message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' = 'success'): void {
    if (this.toastTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.toastTimer);
    }
    this.toastMessage.set(message);
    this.toastType.set(type);
    this.toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.toastMessage.set(null);
    }, 4500);
  }

  closeToast(): void {
    this.toastMessage.set(null);
    if (this.toastTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.toastTimer);
    }
  }

  private extractErrorMessage(err: any, fallback: string): string {
    if (err?.error?.detail) {
      if (typeof err.error.detail === 'string') {
        return err.error.detail;
      }
      if (Array.isArray(err.error.detail) && err.error.detail[0]?.msg) {
        return err.error.detail[0].msg;
      }
    }
    return fallback;
  }
}

export interface TableVersionItem {
  id: string;
  table_id: string;
  version_num: number;
  created_by_username?: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface ColumnSnapshot {
  name: string;
  ordinal_position?: number;
  data_type: string;
  raw_data_type?: string;
  is_nullable?: boolean;
  is_primary_key?: boolean;
  is_foreign_key?: boolean;
  default_value?: string | null;
  classification_level?: string;
  [key: string]: any;
}

export interface ModifiedColumnItem {
  column_name: string;
  v1_attributes: Record<string, any>;
  v2_attributes: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface VersionDiffResponse {
  table_id: string;
  v1_num: number;
  v2_num: number;
  added_columns: ColumnSnapshot[];
  removed_columns: ColumnSnapshot[];
  modified_columns: ModifiedColumnItem[];
}

export type DiffChangeType = 'ADDED' | 'REMOVED' | 'MODIFIED';

export interface DiffSideBySideRow {
  column_name: string;
  change_type: DiffChangeType;
  v1_col: ColumnSnapshot | null;
  v2_col: ColumnSnapshot | null;
  modified_attributes?: {
    attribute: string;
    v1_val: any;
    v2_val: any;
  }[];
}


=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\versioning\services\version.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, map } from 'rxjs';
import { TableVersionItem, VersionDiffResponse } from '../models/version.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class VersionService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/tables';

  /**
   * Retrieves all authorized catalog tables for version comparison.
   */
  getTables(): Observable<Array<{ id: string; name: string; schema_name?: string }>> {
    return this.http.get<{ items: Array<{ id: string; name: string; schema_name?: string }> }>('/api/v1/search?q=').pipe(
      map(res => res?.items || [])
    );
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves chronological list of snapshot versions for a catalog table.
   */
  getVersions(tableId: string): Observable<TableVersionItem[]> {
    return this.http.get<TableVersionItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${tableId}/versions`);
  }

  /**
   * Compares two snapshot versions of a table side-by-side.
   */
  compareVersions(tableId: string, v1: number, v2: number): Observable<VersionDiffResponse> {
    const params = new HttpParams()
      .set('v1', v1.toString())
      .set('v2', v2.toString());

    return this.http.get<VersionDiffResponse>(`${this.baseUrl}/${tableId}/versions/compare`, {
      params,
    });
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\features\versioning\version-diff.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  effect,
  inject,
  input,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { VersionService } from './services/version.service';
import {
  ColumnSnapshot,
  DiffSideBySideRow,
  ModifiedColumnItem,
  TableVersionItem,
  VersionDiffResponse,
} from './models/version.models';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-version-diff',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './version-diff.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./version-diff.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class VersionDiffComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly versionService = inject(VersionService);

  // Inputs using Angular Signals
  readonly diffData = input<VersionDiffResponse | null>(null);
  readonly tableId = input<string | null>(null);
  readonly v1 = input<number | null>(null);
  readonly v2 = input<number | null>(null);
  readonly availableVersions = input<TableVersionItem[]>([]);
  readonly syncJobId = input<string | null>(null);

  // Toolbar & Table Selection State
  readonly availableTables = signal<Array<{ id: string; name: string; schema_name?: string }>>([]);
  readonly selectedTableId = signal<string>('');
  readonly tableVersions = signal<TableVersionItem[]>([]);
  readonly selectedV1 = signal<number>(1);
  readonly selectedV2 = signal<number>(2);

  // State Signals
  readonly fetchedDiff = signal<VersionDiffResponse | null>(null);
  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly activeView = signal<'side-by-side' | 'categorized'>('side-by-side');

  // Computed Diff Resolution
  readonly activeDiff = computed<VersionDiffResponse | null>(() => {
    return this.diffData() ?? this.fetchedDiff();
  });

  readonly addedColumns = computed<ColumnSnapshot[]>(() => {
    return this.activeDiff()?.added_columns ?? [];
  });

  readonly removedColumns = computed<ColumnSnapshot[]>(() => {
    return this.activeDiff()?.removed_columns ?? [];
  });

  readonly modifiedColumns = computed<ModifiedColumnItem[]>(() => {
    return this.activeDiff()?.modified_columns ?? [];
  });

  readonly totalChanges = computed<number>(() => {
    return (
      this.addedColumns().length +
      this.removedColumns().length +
      this.modifiedColumns().length
    );
  });

  readonly isEmpty = computed<boolean>(() => {
    const diff = this.activeDiff();
    return !!diff && this.totalChanges() === 0;
  });

  readonly sideBySideRows = computed<DiffSideBySideRow[]>(() => {
    const diff = this.activeDiff();
    if (!diff) return [];

    const rows: DiffSideBySideRow[] = [];

    // 1. Removed columns (present in v1, absent in v2)
    for (const col of diff.removed_columns) {
      rows.push({
        column_name: col.name,
        change_type: 'REMOVED',
        v1_col: col,
        v2_col: null,
      });
    }

    // 2. Modified columns (present in both with altered attributes)
    for (const mod of diff.modified_columns) {
      const v1Col: ColumnSnapshot = {
        name: mod.column_name,
        data_type: mod.v1_attributes?.['data_type'] ?? 'â€”',
        ...mod.v1_attributes,
      };
      const v2Col: ColumnSnapshot = {
        name: mod.column_name,
        data_type: mod.v2_attributes?.['data_type'] ?? 'â€”',
        ...mod.v2_attributes,
      };

      const diffAttrs = Object.keys(mod.v2_attributes || {}).map((key) => ({
        attribute: key,
        v1_val: mod.v1_attributes[key],
        v2_val: mod.v2_attributes[key],
      }));

      rows.push({
        column_name: mod.column_name,
        change_type: 'MODIFIED',
        v1_col: v1Col,
        v2_col: v2Col,
        modified_attributes: diffAttrs,
      });
    }

    // 3. Added columns (present in v2, absent in v1)
    for (const col of diff.added_columns) {
      rows.push({
        column_name: col.name,
        change_type: 'ADDED',
        v1_col: null,
        v2_col: col,
      });
    }

    return rows;
  });

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      const tid = this.tableId();
      const ver1 = this.v1();
      const ver2 = this.v2();
      const direct = this.diffData();

      if (!direct && tid && ver1 !== null && ver2 !== null) {
        this.loadDiff(tid, ver1, ver2);
      }
    });
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const tid = this.tableId();
    const ver1 = this.v1();
    const ver2 = this.v2();

    if (!this.diffData() && tid && ver1 !== null && ver2 !== null) {
      this.selectedTableId.set(tid);
      this.selectedV1.set(ver1);
      this.selectedV2.set(ver2);
      this.loadDiff(tid, ver1, ver2);
    } else if (!this.diffData()) {
      // Auto-fetch authorized tables and versions on standalone /versions route
      if (typeof this.versionService.getTables === 'function') {
        this.versionService.getTables().subscribe({
          next: (tables) => {
            if (tables && tables.length > 0) {
              this.availableTables.set(tables);
              // Prefer dim_customers if available, otherwise first table
              const defaultTable =
                tables.find((t) => t.name === 'dim_customers') || tables[0];
              this.selectedTableId.set(defaultTable.id);
              this.loadTableVersions(defaultTable.id);
            }
          },
          error: () => {},
        });
      }
    }
  }

  loadTableVersions(tableId: string): void {
    if (typeof this.versionService.getVersions === 'function') {
      this.versionService.getVersions(tableId).subscribe({
        next: (versions) => {
          this.tableVersions.set(versions);
          if (versions && versions.length >= 2) {
            const firstVer = versions[0].version_num;
            const latestVer = versions[versions.length - 1].version_num;
            this.selectedV1.set(firstVer);
            this.selectedV2.set(latestVer);
            this.loadDiff(tableId, firstVer, latestVer);
          } else if (versions && versions.length === 1) {
            this.selectedV1.set(versions[0].version_num);
            this.selectedV2.set(versions[0].version_num);
            this.loadDiff(tableId, versions[0].version_num, versions[0].version_num);
          }
        },
        error: () => {},
      });
    }
  }

  onTableSelect(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLSelectElement;
    const tid = target.value;
    if (tid) {
      this.selectedTableId.set(tid);
      this.loadTableVersions(tid);
    }
  }

  onV1Select(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLSelectElement;
    this.selectedV1.set(Number(target.value));
  }

  onV2Select(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLSelectElement;
    this.selectedV2.set(Number(target.value));
  }

  triggerComparison(): void {
    const tid = this.selectedTableId();
    const ver1 = this.selectedV1();
    const ver2 = this.selectedV2();
    if (tid && ver1 !== null && ver2 !== null) {
      this.loadDiff(tid, ver1, ver2);
    }
  }

  loadDiff(tableId: string, v1: number, v2: number): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.versionService.compareVersions(tableId, v1, v2).subscribe({
      next: (result) => {
        this.fetchedDiff.set(result);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ…ظ‚ط§غŒط³ظ‡ ظ†ط³ط®ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط§ط³ع©غŒظ…ط§';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  setViewMode(mode: 'side-by-side' | 'categorized'): void {
    this.activeView.set(mode);
  }

  getAttributeDiffs(mod: ModifiedColumnItem): { attribute: string; v1_val: any; v2_val: any }[] {
    const keys = new Set([
      ...Object.keys(mod.v1_attributes || {}),
      ...Object.keys(mod.v2_attributes || {}),
    ]);
    return Array.from(keys).map((key) => ({
      attribute: key,
      v1_val: mod.v1_attributes?.[key],
      v2_val: mod.v2_attributes?.[key],
    }));
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\datacatalog\frontend\src\app\app.component.ts ===
import {
  Component,
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  inject,
  signal,
  ElementRef,
  HostListener,
  viewChild,
} from "@angular/core";

import {
  RouterOutlet,
  RouterLink,
  RouterLinkActive,
  Router,
} from "@angular/router";
import { ScrollingModule } from "@angular/cdk/scrolling";
import { AuthService } from "./core/auth/auth.service";
import { RbacService } from "./core/auth/rbac.service";
import { HasPermissionDirective } from "./core/directives/has-permission.directive";
import { CatalogStore } from "./features/catalog/stores/catalog.store";

@Component({
  selector: "app-root",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    RouterOutlet,
    RouterLink,
    RouterLinkActive,
    ScrollingModule,
    HasPermissionDirective,
  ],
  templateUrl: "./app.component.html",
  styleUrl: "./app.component.css",
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class AppComponent {
  readonly authService = inject(AuthService);
  readonly rbacService = inject(RbacService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);
  private readonly catalogStore = inject(CatalogStore);

  readonly title = signal<string>("Enterprise Data Catalog");
  readonly persianTitle = signal<string>("ط³ط§ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‡ ط¬ط§ظ…ط¹ ع©ط§طھط§ظ„ظˆع¯ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ");
  readonly isSidebarOpen = signal<boolean>(true);
  readonly currentVersion = signal<string>("1.0.0");

  readonly isRoleDropdownOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isSwitchingRole = signal<boolean>(false);

  readonly globalSearchInputRef =
    viewChild<ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>>("globalSearchInput");
  readonly globalSearchQuery = signal<string>("");

  @HostListener("window:keydown", ["$event"])
  handleGlobalShortcut(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
      event.preventDefault();
      this.focusGlobalSearch();
    }
  }

  focusGlobalSearch(): void {
    const globalSearchInputRef = this.globalSearchInputRef();
    if (globalSearchInputRef?.nativeElement) {
      globalSearchInputRef.nativeElement.focus();
      globalSearchInputRef.nativeElement.select();
    }
  }

  onGlobalSearchInput(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    this.globalSearchQuery.set(target ? target.value : "");
  }

  executeGlobalSearch(): void {
    const query = this.globalSearchQuery().trim();
    this.router.navigate(["/catalog"], {
      queryParams: query ? { q: query } : {},
    });
  }

  toggleSidebar(): void {
    this.isSidebarOpen.update((open) => !open);
  }

  toggleRoleDropdown(): void {
    this.isRoleDropdownOpen.update((open) => !open);
  }

  switchRole(roleType: "admin" | "steward" | "analyst"): void {
    const creds = {
      admin: { username: "admin", password: "Admin#2026" },
      steward: { username: "steward", password: "Steward#2026" },
      analyst: { username: "analyst", password: "Analyst#2026" },
    }[roleType];

    this.isSwitchingRole.set(true);
    this.isRoleDropdownOpen.set(false);

    this.authService
      .login({ username_or_email: creds.username, password: creds.password })
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.isSwitchingRole.set(false);
          // Refresh catalog store to immediately reflect RBAC filter
          this.catalogStore.search("", true).catch(() => {});
          // Reload current active route so Trees, Connections, IAM, Audit Logs, and Reports refresh immediately
          const currentUrl = this.router.url;
          this.router
            .navigateByUrl("/", { skipLocationChange: true })
            .then(() => {
              this.router.navigateByUrl(currentUrl);
            });
        },
        error: () => {
          this.isSwitchingRole.set(false);
        },
      });
  }

  logout(): void {
    this.authService.logout();
    this.router.navigate(["/login"]);
  }
}

// Alias for compatibility
export { AppComponent as App };

import { ApplicationConfig, provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners } from '@angular/core';
import { provideRouter } from '@angular/router';
import { provideHttpClient, withInterceptors } from '@angular/common/http';
import { provideAnimationsAsync } from '@angular/platform-browser/animations/async';
import { providePrimeNG } from 'primeng/config';
import Aura from '@primeng/themes/aura';

import { routes } from './app.routes';
import { authInterceptor } from './core/auth/auth.interceptor';

export const appConfig: ApplicationConfig = {
  providers: [
    provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners(),
    provideRouter(routes),
    provideHttpClient(withInterceptors([authInterceptor])),
    provideAnimationsAsync(),
    providePrimeNG({
      theme: {
        preset: Aura,
        options: {
          darkModeSelector: false,
        },
      },
      ripple: true,
    }),
  ],
};
